The normalized catalogue preserves every original ID, language, owner, name, kind, scope, source URL and audited status. Query it locally without loading the full list in the public page:

Changed implementation files make the audit a visibly dated snapshot; documentation changes are reported separately. Later working-tree reviews in the original audit are not promoted to shipped implementation or fresh test passes.

## Evidence rules

### AS2-INPUT-HTML5-20261027T0140Z — Independent HTML5 acceptance: AS2-INPUT-HTML5-20261027T0140Z

accepted bounded as2 class input family pointer P0 closed in observed route

All per-declaration changes remain partial; successful authored project behavior does not establish full AVM1 classes, prototype enumeration, erased annotation semantics or every default/return contract. Browser logical resize, Stage listener removal, Key/Mouse listener removal, printable ASCII/layout rules, full input focus and multi-touch/secondary-pointer behavior were not exercised. Stage.addListener is not promoted solely because registration returned without an error. Only Codex in-app browser was checked; engine/version not separately identified and original Flash not run: documented_only. Native Sources/undo/save-reopen/D-008 remain pending; the unsaved native document was preserved. Geometry/timer/ByteArray work after a3fedc3 receives no inherited pass. Older independently measured builds and their failures remain historical evidence. Owner-aware diagnostics outside the bounded text/input profile, cross-handler typed slots/defaults and AS2 Number conversion remain open; rich text/assets and JS/TS project modules remain outside this verified family.

- `as2:statements.html#class` — **partial**: Three qualified AS2 classes compile and attach to authored root/instances; two Widget instances initialize and update independently. Root document_class is a NeoFlash extension, not historical AS2 document-class compatibility.
- `as2:statements.html#extends` — **partial**: Widget extends Base extends MovieClip; inherited activation/reset and base construction produce two independent counts with shared total. Deeper inheritance and full AVM1 construction order were not verified.
- `as2:statements.html#import` — **partial**: Exact import app.Base resolves the superclass and shared static state in this three-source project. Wildcard imports, cross-dialect imports and ambiguous resolution are outside the accepted scope.
- `as2:statements.html#public` — **partial**: Public variables and methods in the bounded AS2 class profile compile and execute in the linked-instance scenario. Nonpublic access control and reflection were not verified.
- `as2:statements.html#static` — **partial**: Base.instances is 2 and Base.total starts at 0; two clicks and two arrow actions produce shared total 4 with independent counts 2/2. Restart restores instances 2 and total 0. Static dependency order and inherited static edge cases were not verified.
- `as2:statements.html#super` — **partial**: Explicit constructor super() initializes the Base/MovieClip chain before the two widgets become interactive. super.method calls, implicit constructors and super value extraction were not exercised.
- `as2:Key.html#addListener()` — **partial**: The root listener receives one callback per observed arrow/H/S/Space action, including after Restart with no duplicate activation. addListener return value, duplicate registration and listener-list mutation were not tested in this browser run.
- `as2:Key.html#event:onKeyDown` — **partial**: Root onKeyDown activates the matching left/right instance and updates visible status; Space resets both counts and shared total. IME, repeat, modifier and focus-edge behavior were not exercised.
- `as2:Key.html#event:onKeyUp` — **partial**: Releasing Left/Right/H/S/Space invokes onKeyUp with codes 37/39/72/83/32 and isDown(code) false. Layout and modifier semantics remain unverified.
- `as2:Key.html#isDown()` — **partial**: LEFT/RIGHT are down when their key-down callbacks activate the corresponding card; key-up traces report false for the released code. Multiple simultaneously held keys and blur clearing were not tested.
- `as2:Key.html#getCode()` — **partial**: Key-up traces report 37/39/72/83/32; getCode selects Space reset and H/S cursor operations. Broader key-layout mapping and held-key ordering were not tested.
- `as2:Key.html#getAscii()` — **partial**: The screenshot after ArrowRight shows Key 39 / ASCII 0 / shared total 4. Printable ASCII, control-character mapping and historical Flash keyboard remapping were not tested.
- `as2:Key.html#LEFT` — **partial**: Real Left input activates only the left card; its release reports code 37. No other constant or keyboard layout is inferred.
- `as2:Key.html#RIGHT` — **partial**: Real Right input activates only the right card; its release reports code 39. No other constant or keyboard layout is inferred.
- `as2:Key.html#SPACE` — **partial**: Real Space input produces AS2 reset 0 0 0 and release code 32. No other key constant is inferred.
- `as2:Mouse.html#addListener()` — **partial**: Registering the root enables one global mouse-down callback per observed coordinate press. After Restart the first press is mouse 1 without duplicate listener delivery. Registration return value and mutation/removal were not tested.
- `as2:Mouse.html#event:onMouseDown` — **partial**: Each real card press logs one AS2 mouse callback before one inherited component activation. Mouse-up/move/wheel and button-edge behavior were not tested.
- `as2:Mouse.html#hide()` — **partial**: After H, the browser records #stage style cursor: none. This establishes stage-local cursor hiding; previous-visibility return values and OS-global cursor behavior were not tested.
- `as2:Mouse.html#show()` — **partial**: After S, the browser records #stage style cursor: pointer. This establishes restoration of the host cursor policy; previous-visibility return values were not tested.
- `as2:Stage.html#width` — **partial**: Startup and Restart report logical Stage width 960 in the fixed fitted publication. Width writes, noScale/window resizing and resize callbacks were not tested.
- `as2:Stage.html#height` — **partial**: Startup and Restart report logical Stage height 540 in the fixed fitted publication. Height writes, noScale/window resizing and resize callbacks were not tested.
- `as2:MovieClip.html#event:onPress` — **partial**: Inherited AS2 prototype onPress receives real coordinate presses and invokes the correct instance method once; independent counts and shared state reset on Restart. Prototype replacement and own callback suppression remain integration-only evidence.
- `as2:MovieClip.html#_x` — **partial**: Class activation changes the corresponding card position through _x while its sibling retains independent state; Space restores the authored positions. Numeric/coercion edge cases were not independently exercised.
- `as2:MovieClip.html#_name` — **partial**: The two linked AS2 instances read authored names left/right in traces and render LEFT / n and RIGHT / n captions. Renaming, unusual names and name-lookup mutation were not tested.
- `AS3:method:flash/events/EventDispatcher.html#addEventListener()` — **partial**: On a3fedc3, real coordinate clicks now reach linked AS3 class and Stage listeners exactly once. Stage capture records phase 1 before the pad listener records phase 3; re-add after removal and Restart work. This corrects the observed FAMILY-INPUT-001 route for the tested in-app browser only.
- `AS3:method:flash/events/EventDispatcher.html#removeEventListener()` — **partial**: Clicking the Stage scenario toggle removes the pad callback; the following real pad click produces no increment. Re-add permits Pad 2, and Restart returns to Pad 1. Mutation during dispatch and full removal semantics were not tested.
- `AS3:method:flash/events/EventDispatcher.html#hasEventListener()` — **partial**: The Stage toggle reports Pad listener false after removal and true after re-add, matching callback suppression/resumption on real clicks. Other event types and ancestor listeners were not tested.
- `AS3:constant:flash/events/MouseEvent.html#CLICK` — **partial**: CLICK listeners receive actual coordinate clicks in linked AS3 instances and the Stage scenario on the corrected guard build. Repeated clicks/Restart do not duplicate callbacks; touch, pen and other engines remain unverified.
- `AS3:constant:flash/events/KeyboardEvent.html#KEY_DOWN` — **partial**: The Stage scenario receives real ArrowRight KEY_DOWN, reports code 39 and moves pad x from 60 to 70. It remains a bounded keyboard regression control, not full keyboard conformance.
- `AS3:property:flash/events/Event.html#eventPhase` — **partial**: Real Stage scenario clicks report capture phase 1 followed by pad listener phase 3, including after Restart. Target phase 2, stopping propagation and redispatch were not exercised by this scenario.

### AUDIO-HTML5-20261027T0429Z — Independent HTML5 acceptance: AUDIO-HTML5-20261027T0429Z

accepted bounded silent mp3 loading channels completion stop restart

All authored channels remained at volume zero. This run establishes native media-clock/events and bounded data/ownership paths, not perceived quality, PCM equivalence, audible panning or Flash mixer parity. The ordinary master bus activates normally; silence here comes from channel gains, not a permanent muted master probe. Only the original MP3, AS3 embedded load and AS2 same-origin HTTP/cache-backed load were observed. Native WAV decode, other codecs, streaming, ID3, malformed files, CORS/redirects and load-error callbacks remain unverified by this run. AS3 zero offset/two repeats and AS2 zero offset/one total play were exercised. Nonzero offsets, arbitrary repeat/coercion/volume ranges, mid-play position accuracy, Sound.close/pending-decode races and budget exhaustion are not accepted. Linkage attachSound, native Sound subclassing, sampleData/extract, four-channel transform matrices, SoundMixer/hierarchy mixing and authored audio timeline synchronization are outside the supported profile. Only observed declarations receive partial overrides. Other Sound members remain at their existing audit status. Original Flash and other engines were not run (documented_only). Native audio/Library integration and Sources/D-008 remain pending; the unsaved native document was untouched. The earlier direct-file URL-policy refusal was not retried. The main chat is developing Date, JSON, RegExp and related String work; later source changes inherit no browser pass from this audio snapshot.

- `AS3:constructor:flash/media/Sound.html#Sound()` — **partial**: AS3 no-argument Sound construction in an ordinary HTML5 publication loaded the embedded 4727-byte MP3 and reached real browser decode completion. No native subclassing or loader-context claim.
- `AS3:method:flash/media/Sound.html#load()` — **partial**: Complete-file URLRequest GET of audio/original-pulse.mp3 resolved the embedded 4727-byte MP3 and invoked the AS3 complete listener after the shipped browser decoder route. Streaming and arbitrary formats remain outside this observation.
- `AS3:property:flash/media/Sound.html#bytesLoaded` — **partial**: The embedded MP3 reports 4727 encoded bytes after actual load/decode completion, including after Restart; this does not measure decoded PCM memory.
- `AS3:property:flash/media/Sound.html#bytesTotal` — **partial**: The embedded MP3 reports 4727 encoded bytes in trace and visible Ready state, including after Restart.
- `AS3:property:flash/media/Sound.html#length` — **partial**: The browser-decoded MP3 duration rounds to 700 milliseconds after actual completion. This is the observed codec/browser result, not a timing-precision or historical Flash parity claim.
- `AS3:event:flash/media/Sound.html#event:complete` — **partial**: The registered Sound COMPLETE listener runs after actual embedded MP3 decode, displays Ready state and runs again for the fresh Sound after Restart; failure/event-order variants remain unverified.
- `AS3:constant:flash/events/Event.html#COMPLETE` — **partial**: COMPLETE is used to register the Sound load listener that actually runs; other event owners are outside this observation.
- `as2:Sound.html#Sound()` — **partial**: No-argument AS2 Sound construction loads /pulse.mp3 into an independent adapter through same-origin HTTP/cache transport. MovieClip target hierarchy is unsupported.
- `as2:Sound.html#loadSound()` — **partial**: loadSound("/pulse.mp3", false) completes through same-origin HTTP/cache transport and native MP3 decode with onLoad(true), byte counts 4727/4727 and duration rounded to 700 milliseconds. Server evidence includes initial HTTP 200 then 304 revalidation for standalone/Restart. Streaming remains unsupported.
- `as2:Sound.html#event:onLoad` — **partial**: Successful native MP3 decode through same-origin HTTP/cache transport invokes the authored onLoad(true) callback with this referring to the Sound adapter; fresh loading after Restart also succeeds. Failure callback semantics were not exercised.
- `as2:Sound.html#getBytesTotal()` — **partial**: The successfully loaded /pulse.mp3 reports 4727 encoded bytes in its visible Ready card and trace, including after Restart.
- `AS3:method:flash/media/Sound.html#play()` — **partial**: Actual authored play(0, 2, SoundTransform(0, 0.25)) runs a silent AS3 channel with two repeats and one final completion; elapsed traces were 2171 and 2108 milliseconds for a duration rounded to 700 milliseconds. This supports the observed three-rendition lifecycle only, not exact timing, nonzero offsets, audible output or general loop fidelity.
- `AS3:constructor:flash/media/SoundTransform.html#SoundTransform()` — **partial**: Constructs a finite zero-volume transform with pan 0.25 for actual silent playback. Constructor range/coercion variants and channel-matrix behavior are unverified.
- `AS3:property:flash/media/SoundTransform.html#volume` — **partial**: Actual channel transform readback is zero. Assigning 2 to the detached transform raises RangeError and leaves that transform volume zero; valid silent playback still completes. No audible gain-quality or full Flash coercion claim.
- `AS3:property:flash/media/SoundTransform.html#pan` — **partial**: Changing the detached transform pan to -0.5 leaves the current channel pan at 0.25. Assigning that valid silent transform to the channel then reads -0.5 and playback completes. Audible panning and historical mixer parity are unverified.
- `AS3:property:flash/media/SoundChannel.html#soundTransform` — **partial**: A detached getter result can change pan to -0.5 while the channel remains at 0.25. Setting that silent transform back yields channel volume 0 and pan -0.5, with actual completion preserved. No wider transform/matrix compatibility claim.
- `AS3:property:flash/media/SoundChannel.html#position` — **partial**: Observed position is 0 immediately after play and rounds to 700 milliseconds at final natural completion, including after Restart. Continuous native-clock progress, per-loop position and timing precision were not measured.
- `AS3:event:flash/media/SoundChannel.html#event:soundComplete` — **partial**: The shipped native source completion route invokes the authored listener exactly once after the three-rendition AS3 playback; explicit Stop and Restart before completion suppress old callbacks, and a fresh session completes once.
- `AS3:constant:flash/events/Event.html#SOUND_COMPLETE` — **partial**: SOUND_COMPLETE is used to register the SoundChannel listener that actually receives natural completion.
- `as2:Sound.html#start()` — **partial**: Actual start(0, 1) runs one silent AS2 rendition and completes approximately 0.75 seconds after the play trace, separately from the AS3 three-rendition channel. Nonzero second offsets, multiple total plays and exact timing are unverified.
- `as2:Sound.html#setVolume()` — **partial**: AS2 setVolume(0) is applied before the actual silent playback and readback remains zero; arbitrary gain/coercion and hierarchy mixing remain outside scope.
- `as2:Sound.html#getVolume()` — **partial**: AS2 getVolume() returns zero in the visible playback and completion cards after actual native playback.
- `as2:Sound.html#setPan()` — **partial**: AS2 setPan(-25) is applied to the adapter-owned silent channel and actual playback still completes. No audible stereo or hierarchy-mixer fidelity claim.
- `as2:Sound.html#getPan()` — **partial**: AS2 getPan() returns -25 in the visible playback and completion cards.
- `as2:Sound.html#event:onSoundComplete` — **partial**: Natural native completion invokes the authored AS2 callback exactly once for the observed single-rendition channel. Stop and Restart before completion suppress the old callback; the fresh session completes once.
- `AS3:method:flash/media/SoundChannel.html#stop()` — **partial**: Stopping the second silent AS3 run approximately 33 milliseconds after its play trace leaves completion count unchanged beyond the prior end time. Restart before another completion suppresses the old session and a fresh run completes once; no direct stale-reference or pending-decode close test is implied.
- `as2:Sound.html#stop()` — **partial**: AS2 stop() stops this adapter-owned silent run and suppresses its natural completion beyond the prior deadline; the independent AS3 channel is controlled separately. Document-wide mixer and linkage-identifier behavior are not implied.
- `AS3:property:flash/media/Sound.html#url` — **partial**: After actual embedded MP3 load completion, url reads audio/original-pulse.mp3, including after Restart. Redirected/security-sensitive URL behavior remains unverified.
- `as2:Sound.html#getBytesLoaded()` — **partial**: After successful /pulse.mp3 HTTP/cache-backed native decode, getBytesLoaded reports 4727 encoded bytes, including after Restart.
- `as2:Sound.html#duration` — **partial**: After successful native MP3 decode, AS2 duration rounds to 700 milliseconds. This is a measured browser/codec result, not exact historical Flash timing.
- `as2:Sound.html#position` — **partial**: In both observed natural completion callbacks, the owned AS2 channel position rounds to 700 milliseconds. Intermediate progress and per-loop position are unverified.

### B01 — Architectural absence of platform libraries

Bounded per-handler AS frontend exposes only its accepted core calls and small display proxy. No native Flash/AIR/Flex/component class library, project resolver/linker or generic VM is bundled. Authored NeoFlash geometry/events are not arbitrary callable Flash APIs. This is an architectural absence inference, not an individual execution test.

Architectural inference; declarations were not individually executed.

### BITMAP-HTML5-20261027T0345Z — Independent HTML5 acceptance: BITMAP-HTML5-20261027T0345Z

accepted bounded PNG shared pixels AS2 attachment disposal and Restart

Only 24 exact positively observed declarations become partial; the AS3 BitmapData constructor receives needs_review from diagnostic-only evidence. No class-wide promotion. PNG at integer scale only. JPEG, smoothing/snapping behavior, alpha-hit routing, mergeAlpha/clipping/overlap, setter reassignment, opaque buffers, all coercions and original Flash rasterization are not accepted. Two exploratory clicks on a listener attached directly to the authored Bitmap produced no callback. Frozen runtime routes noninteractive bitmap owners to ancestors; this does not test alpha hits or establish a pointer regression. General Loader, BitmapData.loadBitmap, draw, filters, bitmap fills, encoding and lock/unlock remain unsupported by the documented profile; no new browser diagnostic for those methods was run. Native image placement/Library, Undo/clipboard, Sources/D-008 and direct-file opening remain pending. The unsaved native document was untouched; the earlier file-protocol denial was not retried or bypassed. Later dynamic TextField/TextFormat source changes are outside this frozen batch and inherit no pass.

- `AS3:constructor:flash/display/Bitmap.html#Bitmap()` — **partial**: Three Bitmap views are created from live buffers; two display the same shared16×16 buffer and one displays an independent clone. Integer transforms and nearest-neighbor rendering work in this scene. Empty/default/null construction, subclassing and all constructor coercions are unverified.
- `AS3:property:flash/display/Bitmap.html#bitmapData` — **partial**: The authored PNG exposes its BitmapData; left/right view reads compare equal to shared while the clone view differs. Pixel mutation changes both shared views without changing the PNG or independent clone. Setter reassignment/null disposal behavior is not established.
- `AS3:method:flash/display/BitmapData.html#clone()` — **partial**: Cloning an authored16×16 PNG preserves its sampled pixels; the second clone owns independent pixels after scroll/copy and remains unchanged by shared-view paint. Full allocation/coercion and every alpha case are unverified.
- `AS3:method:flash/display/BitmapData.html#fillRect()` — **partial**: Integer rectangle fill creates the gold4×4 patch and paints the shared8×6 center orange then purple, visibly updating both views. Original and independent clone retain their contents. Clipped/negative/fractional and partial-alpha fills are outside this pass.
- `AS3:method:flash/display/BitmapData.html#setPixel32()` — **partial**: Setting(15,15) to0xfff08080 creates the visible salmon pixel in shared views. Other alpha values, transparent-to-opaque conversion and out-of-bounds behavior are unverified.
- `AS3:method:flash/display/BitmapData.html#scroll()` — **partial**: The independent clone scrolls2 pixels right, leaves its exposed gold edge unchanged at(0,0), and displays the shifted image before a separate copy operation. Other directions, extreme shifts and full transparent-edge behavior are unverified.
- `AS3:method:flash/display/BitmapData.html#copyPixels()` — **partial**: Copying PNG rectangle(4,3,8,8) to clone point(0,8) gives expected0xff2ad4a5 at(0,8) and(7,15), with a distinct visible clone. Masks, mergeAlpha, overlapping copy and clipping are not browser-accepted.
- `AS3:method:flash/display/BitmapData.html#setPixel()` — **partial**: Painting sets RGB(0,0)=0x1f3045 while preserving the existing opaque alpha: getPixel32 returns0xff1f3045. Fractional coordinates, nonopaque alpha and invalid color conversion remain unverified.
- `AS3:method:flash/display/BitmapData.html#getPixel32()` — **partial**: Selected transparent/opaque PNG and clone samples match independently decoded bytes, and paint reports0xffff965c then0xff8b9dff. A disposed clone read gives the explicit diagnostic. Exhaustive pixel/alpha and original Flash rounding equivalence are not claimed.
- `AS3:method:flash/display/BitmapData.html#getPixel()` — **partial**: After setPixel, reading(0,0) returns RGB0x1f3045 without alpha, beside getPixel32=0xff1f3045. Transparent/out-of-range reads and full coordinate conversion are unverified.
- `AS3:property:flash/display/BitmapData.html#width` — **partial**: The shared16-pixel buffer width appears in the actual status and ready trace. Other dimensions, setters and allocation boundaries are not accepted by this getter observation.
- `AS3:property:flash/display/BitmapData.html#height` — **partial**: The shared16-pixel buffer height appears in the actual status and ready trace. Other dimensions, setters and allocation boundaries are not accepted by this getter observation.
- `AS3:property:flash/display/BitmapData.html#transparent` — **partial**: The decoded PNG buffer reports transparent=true while a selected empty pixel reads0. The opaque BitmapData mode and read-only enforcement are not tested.
- `AS3:property:flash/display/BitmapData.html#rect` — **partial**: The original PNG rect.width reads16 through its returned geometry object. Full rect coordinates, clone independence and mutation/writeback semantics are not tested.
- `AS3:method:flash/display/BitmapData.html#dispose()` — **partial**: Disposing the independent displayed clone removes its pixels and makes a subsequent getPixel32 read fail explicitly. Both shared views still paint, and Restart recreates the clone. Idempotence, shared-buffer disposal and all stale-reference operations are unverified.
- `as2:MovieClip.html#attachBitmap()` — **partial**: Append-depth attachBitmap renders the new AS2 pixel buffer under its own MovieClip at scale600%. It shows the green base, orange rectangle and changed top-left pixel. Arbitrary depth replacement, return value, smoothing/snapping behavior and pointer dispatch are unverified.
- `as2:flash/display/BitmapData.html#BitmapData()` — **partial**: A valid new BitmapData(16,16,true,0xff2ad4a5) creates the visible AS2 buffer; getters report16/16/true. Defaults, coercions, limits and historical invalid-constructor behavior are unverified.
- `as2:flash/display/BitmapData.html#fillRect()` — **partial**: Filling rectangle(4,4,8,8) with0xffff965c produces the visible orange center in the AS2 attached image. Clipping, alpha and alternate rectangle forms are not accepted.
- `as2:flash/display/BitmapData.html#setPixel32()` — **partial**: Setting(0,0) to0xff122233 changes the AS2 buffer; its readback equals4279378483 and the image is rendered. Other alpha/coercion/out-of-bounds behavior is unverified.
- `as2:flash/display/BitmapData.html#getPixel32()` — **partial**: The actual AS2 trace reads(0,0)=0xff122233 after its write, beside the visible attached image. Other pixel cases and historical disposed sentinels are unverified.
- `as2:flash/display/BitmapData.html#rectangle` — **partial**: The AS2 rectangle alias returns width16 and is used in visible status and trace. Other fields, mutation, identity and rect alias equivalence are not exhaustively tested.
- `as2:flash/display/BitmapData.html#width` — **partial**: The created AS2 buffer reports width16 in its trace. Other sizes and writes are unverified.
- `as2:flash/display/BitmapData.html#height` — **partial**: The created AS2 buffer reports height16 in its trace. Other sizes and writes are unverified.
- `as2:flash/display/BitmapData.html#transparent` — **partial**: The AS2 constructor true argument is read back as true on the rendered buffer. Opaque mode and property writes are unverified.

### C01 — Accepted operators use JavaScript value/coercion semantics

Arithmetic, comparison, equality, truth conversion, typeof and untyped writes emit the corresponding JavaScript operation. Selected AS3 named-binding coercions are separate; no AVM1/AVM2 value model or player-version dispatch is present. typeof on a host display-object proxy is not a compiler-level MovieClip type test.

static inspection of snapshot plus explicitly listed Node compiler probes; no original Flash or browser differential verification

### C02 — AS3 typed slots are enforced only on bare names, and handler defaults are late

var count:int=0; this.count=2147483648; observed=count compiles and yields 2147483648. before=count; var count:int yields undefined before declaration, 0 afterwards. Function-local int defaults are correctly established at entry in the selected probe. AS2 annotations are erased. Object/Array/custom annotations are rejected, even though corresponding literals can be used untyped.

### C03 — Core conversion functions are forwarded to host JavaScript

AS2 parseInt("0777") and Number("0777") compile and both return 777 in Node. The selected Adobe AS2 parseInt example specifies 511; its Number reference also specifies octal interpretation of leading-zero strings. No target-player version parameter exists in compile(Script).

### C04 — Whitelisted core methods are native JavaScript calls, with a confirmed documented range mismatch

Calls are permitted by method spelling and forwarded to the receiver. AS3 Number.toFixed(21) returns a 21-digit fraction and does not enter catch(RangeError), whereas the Adobe contract permits 0..20 and specifies RangeError outside it. Receiver-specific argument coercion, range/errors and full method semantics are not implemented by the allowlist.

### C10 — Functions, lexical locals and closures exist within a restricted receiver/call model

Direct function declarations are hoisted; local var defaults and closure capture are implemented. AS3 top-level declarations bind this to the frame owner. Explicit this inside AS2 or nested/expression functions is rejected. Handler bare names become instance fields, local bindings use lexical JS variables. AS3 exact arity checks throw JS TypeError; defaults/rest/arguments and block function declarations are unsupported. Callable member discovery requires same resolved static receiver path within a script; computed calls and alias inference are rejected.

### C11 — Synchronous exceptions and selected Error facades implement a bounded subset

AS3 throws raw values, ordered catches use primitive/core Error predicates, catch scopes are lexical and finally remains a native construct. AS2 explicit throws use branded envelopes; native JS/host errors bypass AS2 catch. AS2 catch supports only untyped or Error and rejects shadowing/catch closures. Arbitrary constructors/classes, boxed/custom catch identity, error codes, stack traces and cross-language interoperability are incomplete.

### CLIPPING-HTML5-20261027T0652Z — Independent HTML5 acceptance: CLIPPING-HTML5-20261027T0652Z

accepted bounded vector masks and integral scroll with live instances

Parent viewport right-edge discrimination is not independently established: authored phase1 target ends atx945 before viewport edge980. Nested scene works within the observed overlap only. Fractional/deferred scroll normalization, mask source reuse/retirement, arbitrary dynamic mutations, owner reentry, quotas/depth, pending operations and old wrappers remain untested. No timeline-animated masks or bitmap/text/alpha mask acceptance. Three visible Open card semantic entries occur in each saved AX snapshot; same names do not identify instances or prove full keyboard/screenreader behavior. No original Flash or cross-engine run. All pixel comparisons refer only to the saved IAB screenshot profile; no exact Flash rasterization/antialiasing claim. AS2rgb, resource overlay, Native Sources/D-008, handler defaults, AS2 Number and owner-aware guards are not retested/closed. Unsaved native document untouched; blocked file URL route not retried. Current main work is image loading via Loader/LoaderInfo/MovieClipLoader. Later live sources receive no passes from this frozen05d5d43 acceptance. Earlier runs and baseline remain separate.

- `AS3:property:flash/display/DisplayObject.html#mask` — **partial**: Actual saved pixels and real input confirm the transparent-filled vector mask has both an even-odd rectangular ring and a circular region. Initial hole clicks produce no panel event; ring and circle produce counts 1 and 2. The translated phase-1 mask retains the hole and allows panel count 3; phase-2 null removal makes the original hole coordinate clickable at count 4; restoring the mask blocks it again. Self-mask assignment is refused with a visible diagnostic and the positive mask continues to work. Panel.mask reads the aperture in clipped phases and null when removed. This accepts the bounded live-object vector mask flow; it does not separately discriminate the parent viewport right edge from absent target content, nor accept alpha masks, bitmap/text sources, animated source timelines, source reuse/retirement or mask budgets.
- `AS3:property:flash/display/DisplayObject.html#scrollRect` — **partial**: Actual phases 0/1/2/0 and Restart preserve five linked instances. Card 3 keeps its hit count 1/2/3/4 while its local origin moves through global x 390/235/80/390 at y 248; Restart resets the next hit to 1. All coordinate traces match integral content offsets, global-to-local roundtrip 0/0 and unchanged base matrix translation 60/220. The viewport stays 450 by 230; input outside the visible cards is excluded. Mutating both the supplied Rectangle and a returned copy leaves stored x 0, and fractional x 0.5 raises RangeError alongside the working scene. AS3 absence is null before assignment and after right-viewport removal. This is immediate integral scrolling with copied state, not historical fractional/deferred normalization or full atomicity beyond the observed retained state/content.
- `as2:MovieClip.html#setMask()` — **partial**: The legacy trapezoid visibly clips the panel and direct live-object assignment reports Boolean true. An excluded point does not trigger onPress while a positive point yields count 1; after phase-2 null removal the same formerly excluded point yields count 2; restoration excludes it again. The return of the null call was not separately traced. This observed Boolean direct-object profile is distinguished from the documentation Void signature; no original Flash runtime was executed. String paths, missing arguments, invalid receiver behavior and source reuse remain untested.
- `as2:MovieClip.html#scrollRect` — **partial**: The legacy scene begins with native scrollRect===undefined, then reads x 0/width 450 after assigning a Rectangle. Phase1 uses x 55 and shifts the visible stripe; phase2 null removal restores the unclipped drawing and again observes undefined. Positive and negative legacy clicks follow the mask/scroll scene across restoration. This accepts the bounded integral AS2 native-property route; getter-copy behavior is established only for the AS3 viewport in this run, and local variables/ordinary fields, fractional normalization and broader coercion profiles are not promoted.
- `AS3:constant:flash/display/GraphicsPathWinding.html#EVEN_ODD` — **partial**: The constant is used with two same-orientation closed rectangular contours. The initial image has a genuine interior hole and real input is excluded there, while the ring and separate circle component remain clickable. Removing the mask makes that exact hole coordinate visible/clickable; restoring it blocks the point again. This distinguishes the observed EVEN_ODD mask case from filling those contours with nonzero winding. No general self-intersection, orientation or edge-raster equivalence is claimed.
- `AS3:method:flash/display/Graphics.html#drawPath()` — **partial**: Retains GRAPHICS-HTML5-20261027T0619Z acceptance of the Vector MOVE_TO/LINE_TO/CUBIC_CURVE_TO wave and bounded malformed-data refusal. This build additionally exercises ordinary array command/data input for two closed rectangular contours with EVEN_ODD, using transparent filled geometry as a mask. The visible hole and corresponding real-input exclusion, followed by same-coordinate positive input when unmasked, confirm this additional profile. No general path, alpha-mask or exact Flash rasterization claim is made.

### COLLECTIONS-TEXT-HTML5-20261027T0120Z — Independent HTML5 acceptance: COLLECTIONS-TEXT-HTML5-20261027T0120Z

bounded collections plain text verified complete delivery blocked by existing shared pointer click failure

Useful Array/String/plain authored TextField behavior is verified only in the named source scenarios; all affected declarations remain partial. Whole classes are not promoted. The existing shared pointer-click defect remains P0 and prevents full delivery acceptance; the bounded data-to-text results are retained independently. Only Codex in-app browser was exercised; engine/version was not separately identified. Original Flash was not run: documented_only. Array sorting uses ordinary records and bounded inputs. Equal-key casing, historical comparator order, sparse arrays, exotic coercions and complete prototype behavior were not established. TextWidth/textHeight were only checked positive, not compared with original Flash layout. Rich HTML/CSS, wrapping, editable input/selection, embedded fonts and dynamic TextField creation are not implemented by this profile. AS2 host line-query helpers are not promoted to nonexistent native AS2 catalogue entries. Unsupported AS3 htmlText remains missing despite its tested diagnostic. Native Sources, undo/save/reopen and detached Actions D-008 remain pending; no unsaved native document was touched. Later AS2 project classes, classic Key/Mouse/Stage and pointer-guard changes receive no inherited result. Main automated checks are supplemental reported evidence.

- `as2:Array.html#Array()` — **partial**: Empty Array() and new Array() plus multi-value new Array(...) feed the observed record pipeline and numeric sort. Single-length construction, sparse arrays and invalid lengths were not checked.
- `as2:Array.html#length` — **partial**: Dense input length drives three records; slice(0,2).length is 2. Length writes, holes and truncation were not checked.
- `as2:Array.html#push()` — **partial**: Appending record objects and labels produces the final three-entry leaderboard; appended Cy is subsequently returned by pop. New-length return values were not checked.
- `as2:Array.html#splice()` — **partial**: splice(1,1,replacement) replaces zoe with Zoe and returns an array whose first removed record is zoe. Negative indices, variable deletion counts and sparse cases were not checked.
- `as2:Array.html#unshift()` — **partial**: Prepending Ann is observed through the next shift returning Ann and the final leaderboard excluding Ann. The returned length was not checked.
- `as2:Array.html#shift()` — **partial**: After unshift, shift returns the Ann record and removes it from the final leaderboard. Empty arrays and holes were not checked.
- `as2:Array.html#pop()` — **partial**: After push, pop returns the Cy record and removes it from the final leaderboard. Empty arrays and holes were not checked.
- `as2:Array.html#slice()` — **partial**: slice(0,2) yields length 2 while the source still produces three leaderboard entries. Slice contents, negative bounds and returned-array independence were not checked.
- `as2:Array.html#sortOn()` — **partial**: Indexed descending score sort returns 1,0,2 and preserves the first source record Ada; in-place descending score sort returns the source array and produces Zoe:45|Ada:30|Bob:20. Tied secondary keys and accessors were not checked.
- `as2:Array.html#sort()` — **partial**: NUMERIC sorts [30,2,11] to [2,11,30]; UNIQUESORT returns 0 for duplicate echo strings. A per-array sort override returns custom:16. Comparator callbacks, sparse cases and duplicate-array preservation were not checked.
- `as2:Array.html#join()` — **partial**: Explicit pipe, comma and newline separators produce observed leaderboard, index-order and numeric-sort strings. Default separators and null/undefined/hole coercion were not checked.
- `as2:Array.html#reverse()` — **partial**: reverse() returns the exact array receiver (copy === reversed is true). The reversed element order was not logged; ordering correctness is not accepted by this check.
- `as2:Array.html#DESCENDING` — **partial**: Descending score sort produces 45,30,20. Equal-key ordering and combinations beyond the observed indexed/in-place paths were not checked.
- `as2:Array.html#NUMERIC` — **partial**: NUMERIC sorts numeric values [30,2,11] as 2,11,30, and passing the constant to an overridden method prints 16. Numeric strings and mixed values were not checked.
- `as2:Array.html#RETURNINDEXEDARRAY` — **partial**: sortOn returns indices 1,0,2 while the source first record remains Ada. Full source identity/content preservation and tied keys were not checked.
- `as2:Array.html#UNIQUESORT` — **partial**: Sorting two equal echo strings with UNIQUESORT returns 0. The duplicate input content after refusal and the successful unique-input path were not checked.
- `AS3:constructor:Array.html#Array()` — **partial**: Multi-value new Array(...) supplies [30,2,11] to a numeric comparator and duplicate echo strings to UNIQUESORT. Empty/default and exotic argument cases were not checked.
- `AS3:constructor:Array.html#Array():variant:ddbdf02d56c9` — **partial**: new Array(3) accepts subsequent writes at indices 0..2 and those three assigned values feed map. Initial length and holes before those writes, invalid lengths and default construction were not inspected.
- `AS3:property:Array.html#length` — **partial**: After dense mutation records.length is 4 and filter produces selected.length 3. Length writes, truncation and sparse arrays were not checked.
- `AS3:method:Array.html#map()` — **partial**: Callbacks parse three dense input strings and then transform sorted records into the observed labels. Callback thisArg, index/source identity, holes and mutation during iteration were not checked.
- `AS3:method:Array.html#splice()` — **partial**: splice(1,1,replacement) changes zoe to Zoe:45 in the resulting leaderboard. The removed-array return, negative indices and other deletion/insertion forms were not checked.
- `AS3:method:Array.html#push()` — **partial**: Pushing Cy:10 increases records.length to 4 before filtering yields three qualifying records. The new-length return was not checked.
- `AS3:method:Array.html#filter()` — **partial**: The predicate retains the three records scoring at least 20 and excludes Cy:10; selected.length is 3. thisArg, holes and mutation during iteration were not checked.
- `AS3:method:Array.html#sortOn()` — **partial**: Indexed descending score sort returns 1,0,2 while the source first record remains Ada; in-place sorting yields Zoe:45|Ada:30|Bob:20. Tied secondary keys, numeric-versus-lexical discrimination and in-place return identity were not checked.
- `AS3:method:Array.html#forEach()` — **partial**: The callback sums selected scores to 95 and the return has typeof undefined. thisArg, callback index/source identity, holes and mutation during iteration were not checked.
- `AS3:method:Array.html#every()` — **partial**: The all-scores-at-least-20 predicate returns true in the observed combined every/some check. A false result and early termination were not checked.
- `AS3:method:Array.html#some()` — **partial**: The predicate finding Zoe returns true in the observed combined every/some check. A false result, callback count and early-termination order were not checked.
- `AS3:method:Array.html#indexOf()` — **partial**: An exact string search finds Ada:30 at index 1. startIndex, misses, type discrimination and sparse arrays were not checked.
- `AS3:method:Array.html#lastIndexOf()` — **partial**: An exact string search finds Bob:20 at index 2. Repeated matches, startIndex, misses and sparse arrays were not checked.
- `AS3:method:Array.html#sort()` — **partial**: A numeric comparator sorts [30,2,11] to [2,11,30]; UNIQUESORT returns 0 for duplicate echo strings. Native NUMERIC-flag discrimination, comparator call order and duplicate-array preservation were not checked.
- `AS3:method:Array.html#join()` — **partial**: Explicit pipe, comma and newline separators yield observed leaderboard, index-order and sorted-number strings. Default separators and null/undefined/hole coercion were not checked.
- `AS3:constant:Array.html#DESCENDING` — **partial**: Descending score sort produces 45,30,20. Equal-key ordering and other option combinations were not checked.
- `AS3:constant:Array.html#RETURNINDEXEDARRAY` — **partial**: sortOn returns indices 1,0,2 while the source first record remains Ada. Full source identity/content preservation and tied keys were not checked.
- `AS3:constant:Array.html#UNIQUESORT` — **partial**: Sorting two equal echo strings with UNIQUESORT returns 0. Duplicate-input preservation and the successful unique-input path were not checked.
- `as2:String.html#split()` — **partial**: Splitting each name,score input on a literal comma feeds the observed record pipeline. Empty/omitted separators, limits, holes and regular expressions were not checked.
- `AS3:method:String.html#split()` — **partial**: Splitting each name,score input on a literal comma feeds the observed record pipeline. Empty/omitted separators, limits, holes and regular expressions were not checked.
- `as2:String.html#substring()` — **partial**: substring(0,3) selects ada before uppercase conversion yields ADA. Swapped, negative, omitted and out-of-range bounds were not checked.
- `AS3:method:String.html#substring()` — **partial**: substring(0,3) selects ada before uppercase conversion yields ADA. Swapped, negative, omitted and out-of-range bounds were not checked.
- `as2:String.html#toUpperCase()` — **partial**: ASCII ada is converted to ADA. Non-ASCII casing and locale behavior were not checked.
- `AS3:method:String.html#toUpperCase()` — **partial**: ASCII ada is converted to ADA. Non-ASCII casing and locale behavior were not checked.
- `as2:String.html#substr()` — **partial**: substr(4,3) selects zoe. Negative start, omitted length and out-of-range inputs were not checked.
- `AS3:method:String.html#substr()` — **partial**: substr(4,3) selects zoe. Negative start, omitted length and out-of-range inputs were not checked.
- `as2:String.html#toLowerCase()` — **partial**: toLowerCase accepts an already lowercase zoe string and returns zoe. Conversion of uppercase input and Unicode casing were not checked.
- `AS3:method:String.html#toLowerCase()` — **partial**: toLowerCase accepts an already lowercase zoe string and returns zoe. Conversion of uppercase input and Unicode casing were not checked.
- `as2:String.html#slice()` — **partial**: slice(-3) yields bob. Other negative/positive endpoint combinations and omitted arguments were not checked.
- `AS3:method:String.html#slice()` — **partial**: slice(-3) yields bob. Other negative/positive endpoint combinations and omitted arguments were not checked.
- `as2:String.html#charAt()` — **partial**: charAt(0) yields a for the sample string. Out-of-range and surrogate-unit results were not checked.
- `AS3:method:String.html#charAt()` — **partial**: charAt(0) yields a for the sample string. Out-of-range and surrogate-unit results were not checked.
- `as2:String.html#charCodeAt()` — **partial**: charCodeAt(0) yields 97 for a. Out-of-range behavior was not checked.
- `AS3:method:String.html#charCodeAt()` — **partial**: charCodeAt(0) yields 97; reading the emoji-containing text at offsets 1 and 2 yields UTF-16 surrogate units 55357 and 56832. Out-of-range behavior was not checked.
- `as2:String.html#lastIndexOf()` — **partial**: lastIndexOf("|") finds the last of two separators at offset 7. Explicit startIndex and misses were not checked.
- `AS3:method:String.html#lastIndexOf()` — **partial**: lastIndexOf("|") finds the last of two separators at offset 7. Explicit startIndex and misses were not checked.
- `as2:String.html#fromCharCode()` — **partial**: String.fromCharCode(65,66) yields AB. Zero handling, wrapping, surrogate pairs and invalid coercions were not checked.
- `AS3:method:String.html#fromCharCode()` — **partial**: String.fromCharCode(65,66) yields AB. Zero handling, wrapping, surrogate pairs and invalid coercions were not checked.
- `as2:String.html#indexOf()` — **partial**: Searching the returned leaderboard text for CR yields index 6. Explicit startIndex, misses and coercion edge cases were not checked.
- `AS3:method:String.html#indexOf()` — **partial**: Searching the returned leaderboard text for CR yields index 6. Explicit startIndex, misses and coercion edge cases were not checked.
- `AS3:method:String.html#replace()` — **partial**: Literal replace("red","$&") on "red red" yields "$& red": first-match replacement and a literal replacement string. RegExp patterns, callbacks, misses and coercion edge cases were not checked.
- `as2:TextField.html#text` — **partial**: Authored plain TextField receives the three-line leaderboard; getter exposes CR separators (first CR at index 6). Repeated after Restart. Full AS2 coercion and rich text are unverified.
- `as2:TextField.html#textColor` — **partial**: RGB integer 0x70ddfa assigned in the exercised authored-text pipeline and repeated after Restart. General AS2 conversion and formatting semantics are unverified.
- `as2:TextField.html#length` — **partial**: Three-line plain leaderboard reports length 20 before and after Restart. This AS2 observation does not establish all Unicode/coercion cases.
- `AS3:property:flash/text/TextField.html#text` — **partial**: Authored plain-text setter/getter preserves leaderboard text and CR line separators. Unicode A😀B/LF/C reads back as A😀B/CR/C after a rejected split-surrogate edit; saved leaderboard text is restored. Rich text, input and full coercion remain unsupported or unverified.
- `AS3:property:flash/text/TextField.html#textColor` — **partial**: RGB integer 0x48d8b0 assigned in the exercised authored-text pipeline and repeated after Restart. General uint conversion and formatting semantics are unverified.
- `AS3:property:flash/text/TextField.html#length` — **partial**: Leaderboard reports 30 UTF-16 code units; A😀B/LF/C reports 6, including the surrogate pair. Only these authored plain-text cases were observed.
- `AS3:property:flash/text/TextField.html#numLines` — **partial**: Unwrapped leaderboard reports four lines and A😀B/LF/C reports two; repeated after Restart. Wrapping and full Flash layout semantics were not checked.
- `AS3:property:flash/text/TextField.html#textWidth` — **partial**: Canvas-backed width was observed only as textWidth > 0 for the leaderboard. No numerical accuracy, font equivalence or Flash metric comparison was performed.
- `AS3:property:flash/text/TextField.html#textHeight` — **partial**: Renderer-backed height was observed only as textHeight > 0 for the leaderboard. No numerical accuracy, line-layout or Flash metric comparison was performed.
- `AS3:method:flash/text/TextField.html#appendText()` — **partial**: Appends LF plus Total: 95 to the authored leaderboard, producing the observed length 30 and four lines; repeated after Restart. Return value and broader argument conversions were not observed.
- `AS3:method:flash/text/TextField.html#replaceText()` — **partial**: Removes the four-code-unit prefix from the leaderboard. A range splitting the emoji surrogate pair raises RangeError and preserves text. Only bounded ordered UTF-16 edits were observed; return value and broader Flash range behavior were not checked.
- `AS3:method:flash/text/TextField.html#getLineText()` — **partial**: First leaderboard line equals Zoe:45 followed by CR before and after Restart. Other line indices and invalid-index errors were not exercised in this browser run.
- `AS3:method:flash/text/TextField.html#getLineLength()` — **partial**: First leaderboard line including CR reports 7 code units; A😀B followed by CR reports 5. Other ranges and invalid-index errors were not exercised in this browser run.
- `AS3:property:flash/text/TextField.html#htmlText` — **missing**: Setting HTML on an authored TextField raises the explicit plain-text-profile TypeError. A same-named MovieClip custom field remains usable. This closes silent acceptance for this case but does not implement HTML text.

### DATA-HTML5-20261027T0452Z — Independent HTML5 acceptance: DATA-HTML5-20261027T0452Z

accepted bounded resource json regexp utc date report workflow

Only the observed UTC numeric Date workflow is accepted. Current/local/component construction, Date.parse, DST/locale/history, invalid dates, additional methods/accessors and original Flash behavior remain unverified. Independent UTC arithmetic is not a historical Flash oracle. RegExp constructor i/g profiles, exec captures, source and String regex replacement were observed. Other flags, methods, lastIndex, zero-width patterns, pathological native backtracking, full PCRE syntax and slash-literal support were not accepted. Size limits do not prove a hard time bound. JSON reviver numeric conversion, replacer/native refusal, primitive/plain-object parsing and project-class public getter serialization were observed. Cycles, deletion/holes, toJSON, property-order/indentation, deep limits, arbitrary native objects and full coercion/error fidelity remain unverified by this browser run. AS2 JSON and RegExp are outside the supported profile. Diagnostics do not implement unsupported native serialization. Only the24 exact declaration rows receive overrides; no class-wide promotion. Only IAB was tested, engine/version not separately identified; original Flash and other engines were not run (documented_only). Native Sources/D-008 and unsaved-document-dependent UI remain pending; the document was untouched. Direct file URL refusal was not retried. Transform/ColorTransform is already under development in the main chat; later affine/color/alpha edits inherit no browser result from this frozen data build.

- `AS3:constructor:Date.html#Date()` — **partial**: Numeric Date.UTC timestamps construct the three AS3 Date values at 09:30 UTC. Browser output and independently calculated epochs match the observed leap-day, year and month rollover cases; other constructors/local calendar profiles remain unverified.
- `AS3:method:Date.html#UTC()` — **partial**: Numeric UTC components generate the three fixture dates at 09:30 UTC; actual traced epochs match independent Python arithmetic for shifts 0, 1 and 2. Historical local timezone/DST/parsing behavior is outside this scope.
- `AS3:method:Date.html#getUTCDate()` — **partial**: The actual pipeline reads UTC day before applying shift 0, 1 or 2, producing the observed correct leap-day, month and year rollover dates.
- `AS3:method:Date.html#setUTCDate()` — **partial**: Numeric day mutation visibly advances 2024-02-28 to February 29 and March 1, 2026-12-31 to 2027-01-01/02, and 2026-10-31 to November 1/2. Other mutations and coercion boundaries remain unverified.
- `AS3:property:Date.html#fullYearUTC` — **partial**: UTC year readback appears correctly in all three rendered rows, including 2026 to 2027 rollover, and matches the saved numeric traces.
- `AS3:property:Date.html#monthUTC` — **partial**: Zero-based UTC month readback plus one displays the observed February/March, December/January and October/November boundaries correctly.
- `AS3:property:Date.html#dateUTC` — **partial**: UTC day readback correctly renders the observed leap day, March 1, January 1/2 and November 1/2 after numeric mutation.
- `AS3:method:JSON.html#parse()` — **partial**: Parse embedded planning data with a reviver; all three hours values trace as number and total 13. Parse serialized class-report and explicit RegExp-replacer output successfully. Malformed {bad input raises a caught SyntaxError and the positive pipeline continues, including after Restart. General reviver holder/deletion/error-ID behavior is not established.
- `AS3:method:JSON.html#stringify()` — **partial**: Actual serialization includes Report public count/hours plus public getter summary, and its parsed result is 3/13/3 items / 13 hours. Raw RegExp serialization raises the explicit unsupported-native TypeError; an authored replacer returning RegExp.source successfully preserves pattern/count through roundtrip. No general native-object or class-metadata completeness claim.
- `AS3:constructor:RegExp.html#RegExp()` — **partial**: Actual constructor-created case-insensitive anchored code pattern and global whitespace pattern drive the visible data report. Partial native JavaScript backend only; regex literals, arbitrary PCRE syntax and other flags are not accepted by this observation.
- `AS3:method:RegExp.html#exec()` — **partial**: A reused non-global pattern returns full code, alphabetic prefix and numeric suffix for web-104, GAME-205 and anim-306 on every observed render; the captures appear in trace and visible report. Global lastIndex, failure and zero-width cases remain unverified.
- `AS3:method:String.html#replace()` — **partial**: Actual global whitespace RegExp replacement normalizes Build   dashboard, Prototype  controls and Animate   navigation to the visible single-space titles. Replacement callbacks, capture substitutions and wider Unicode rules remain unverified.
- `AS3:method:String.html#split()` — **partial**: Actual primitive hyphen split of each input date feeds three numeric UTC components into the successful rollover pipeline; RegExp splitting remains outside scope.
- `AS3:method:String.html#toUpperCase()` — **partial**: Actual ASCII captures render as WEB, GAME and ANIM alongside their numeric suffixes; no full Unicode case-conversion claim.
- `as2:Date.html#Date()` — **partial**: An independent AS2 Date constructed from numeric Date.UTC shows 2026-10-27 09:30 UTC initially, advances to October 28/29, and restores on Restart. Other constructors remain unverified.
- `as2:Date.html#UTC()` — **partial**: AS2 numeric Date.UTC regenerates the authored 2026-10-27 09:30 UTC base before each independent card update; observed timestamps match independent arithmetic.
- `as2:Date.html#setTime()` — **partial**: AS2 setTime resets to epoch 1793093400000 before applying each shift; visible day progression and Restart sequence 0/1/2/0/1 show no accumulation from previous renders.
- `as2:Date.html#setUTCDate()` — **partial**: Actual AS2 numeric UTC day mutation produces October 27/28/29 for shifts 0/1/2; Restart restores the base and the next click advances once. AS2 rollover boundaries beyond those dates were not exercised.
- `as2:Date.html#getUTCDate()` — **partial**: Actual AS2 UTC day readback supports shift arithmetic and visibly reports October 27/28/29 consistently with independent numeric epochs.
- `as2:Date.html#getUTCFullYear()` — **partial**: Actual AS2 UTC year readback renders 2026 across the observed three dates; AS2 year-boundary behavior was not exercised.
- `as2:Date.html#getUTCMonth()` — **partial**: Actual AS2 zero-based UTC month readback plus one renders October across the observed three dates; AS2 month-boundary behavior was not exercised.
- `as2:Date.html#getTime()` — **partial**: Actual AS2 getTime traces 1793093400000, 1793179800000 and 1793266200000 for shifts 0/1/2, matching independently computed 09:30 UTC timestamps; Restart repeats the corresponding initial and day-one values.
- `AS3:method:Date.html#getTime()` — **partial**: All 15 actual row epoch readings across the pipeline sequence 0/1/2/0/1 exactly match independently computed UTC timestamps for the nine unique row/shift combinations. This is limited numeric UTC evidence, not local timezone or parsing conformance.
- `AS3:property:RegExp.html#source` — **partial**: The explicit JSON replacer reads the constructor pattern source and the parsed encoded result preserves it, alongside count 3; this succeeds before and after Restart while raw RegExp encoding remains diagnosed as unsupported.

### DYNAMIC-BINARY-HTML5-20261027T0220Z — Independent HTML5 acceptance: DYNAMIC-BINARY-HTML5-20261027T0220Z

accepted bounded dynamic display graphics binary workflow with documented limits

Exact tested declaration IDs only; no class-wide or inherited-declaration promotion. Runtime-created children only. Authored-prefix mutations, attachMovie/linkage, linked multi-frame dynamic timelines, depth replacement and complete reentrant Flash event ordering are outside this batch. Solid rectangles/circle/line triangle and bounded stroke only; no gradient, bitmap fill, masks, filters, curve/ellipse conformance or exact raster equivalence. Binary little-endian payload is a same-adapter round trip; only the default-big-endian header is checked against raw expected bytes. External binary interoperability, full allocation/UTF edge cases, native EOFError class/errorID and numeric coercion remain unverified. Compression/AMF/other code pages and AS2 ByteArray remain unsupported. A diagnostic is not an implemented API. Original Flash not executed: documented_only. Only Codex in-app browser checked; engine/version not separately identified. Native Sources/D-008 remains pending; existing unsaved native document untouched. Later source changes inherit no result.

- `AS3:constructor:flash/utils/ByteArray.html#ByteArray()` — **partial**: Three independent no-argument buffers construct the packet/copy pipeline, plus a separate color buffer in dynamic-display. Constructor arguments, subclassing and allocation limits are not positive coverage.
- `AS3:property:flash/utils/ByteArray.html#length` — **partial**: packet.length is 50 and wire.length becomes 0 after clear. EOF read and unsupported compress preserve the relevant buffer length 50. Length-setter growth/truncation and cursor clamping are not exercised.
- `AS3:property:flash/utils/ByteArray.html#position` — **partial**: wire.position=3 and decoded.position=7 are accepted; readBytes preserves destination position7 before decoding from0. A readUnsignedInt overrun leaves decoded.position 50 unchanged, and unsupported compress leaves packet.position 50 unchanged. Arbitrary cursor/coercion cases and unobserved zero-gap bytes remain outside the scope.
- `AS3:property:flash/utils/ByteArray.html#bytesAvailable` — **partial**: After consuming the 50-byte packet, bytesAvailable is0; an added readUnsignedInt overrun leaves it 0 while cursor/length remain 50. Intermediate availability, cursor beyond length and read-only enforcement are not exercised.
- `AS3:property:flash/utils/ByteArray.html#endian` — **partial**: The default header has raw bytes 78,70,66,49; setting LITTLE_ENDIAN permits the matching payload roundtrip. The original fixture does not independently inspect little-endian payload bytes, so exact payload wire order is not established by roundtrip alone.
- `AS3:method:flash/utils/ByteArray.html#writeBoolean()` — **partial**: The packet writes true and the paired reader reproduces it in the browser trace. False/nonzero-byte decoding and coercion are outside this positive example; write return values are not inspected.
- `AS3:method:flash/utils/ByteArray.html#readBoolean()` — **partial**: The paired packet writer/reader roundtrip yields true. False/nonzero-byte decoding and coercion are outside this positive example; symmetric writer/reader errors require independent raw-byte evidence to exclude.
- `AS3:method:flash/utils/ByteArray.html#writeByte()` — **partial**: The packet writes -7 and the paired reader reproduces it in the browser trace. Unsigned boundaries, truncation and coercion are outside this positive example; write return values are not inspected.
- `AS3:method:flash/utils/ByteArray.html#readByte()` — **partial**: The paired packet writer/reader roundtrip yields -7. Unsigned boundaries, truncation and coercion are outside this positive example; symmetric writer/reader errors require independent raw-byte evidence to exclude.
- `AS3:method:flash/utils/ByteArray.html#writeShort()` — **partial**: The packet writes -1024 and the paired reader reproduces it in the browser trace. Full signed range, truncation and raw little-endian payload layout are outside this positive example; write return values are not inspected.
- `AS3:method:flash/utils/ByteArray.html#readShort()` — **partial**: The paired packet writer/reader roundtrip yields -1024. Full signed range, truncation and raw little-endian payload layout are outside this positive example; symmetric writer/reader errors require independent raw-byte evidence to exclude.
- `AS3:method:flash/utils/ByteArray.html#writeInt()` — **partial**: The packet writes -1234567 and the paired reader reproduces it in the browser trace. Full signed range, truncation and raw little-endian payload layout are outside this positive example; write return values are not inspected.
- `AS3:method:flash/utils/ByteArray.html#readInt()` — **partial**: The paired packet writer/reader roundtrip yields -1234567. Full signed range, truncation and raw little-endian payload layout are outside this positive example; symmetric writer/reader errors require independent raw-byte evidence to exclude.
- `AS3:method:flash/utils/ByteArray.html#writeUnsignedInt()` — **partial**: The packet writes default-endian header 1313227313 and payload 4275878552 and the paired reader reproduces it in the browser trace. Overflow/coercion and raw little-endian payload layout are outside this positive example; write return values are not inspected.
- `AS3:method:flash/utils/ByteArray.html#readUnsignedInt()` — **partial**: Positive packet reads yield default-endian header 1313227313 and payload 4275878552. At EOF the added readUnsignedInt probe raises RangeError: ByteArray read exceeds available data, with position 50/length 50/bytesAvailable 0 unchanged; the positive packet still completes and repeats after Restart. Full range/coercion, raw little-endian bytes and Flash EOFError/errorID equivalence are not established.
- `AS3:method:flash/utils/ByteArray.html#writeFloat()` — **partial**: The packet writes 1.25 and the paired reader reproduces it in the browser trace. Rounding boundaries, nan/infinity and raw ieee payload layout are outside this positive example; write return values are not inspected.
- `AS3:method:flash/utils/ByteArray.html#readFloat()` — **partial**: The paired packet writer/reader roundtrip yields 1.25. Rounding boundaries, nan/infinity and raw ieee payload layout are outside this positive example; symmetric writer/reader errors require independent raw-byte evidence to exclude.
- `AS3:method:flash/utils/ByteArray.html#writeDouble()` — **partial**: The packet writes 3.141592653589793 and the paired reader reproduces it in the browser trace. Rounding boundaries, nan/infinity and raw ieee payload layout are outside this positive example; write return values are not inspected.
- `AS3:method:flash/utils/ByteArray.html#readDouble()` — **partial**: The paired packet writer/reader roundtrip yields 3.141592653589793. Rounding boundaries, nan/infinity and raw ieee payload layout are outside this positive example; symmetric writer/reader errors require independent raw-byte evidence to exclude.
- `AS3:method:flash/utils/ByteArray.html#writeUTF()` — **partial**: Writing NeoFlash π 🦀 and reading it back preserves BMP/supplementary Unicode in visible text; the total packet is 50 bytes. Raw length prefix, BOM/NUL, malformed input and 65535-byte limit are not positive coverage.
- `AS3:method:flash/utils/ByteArray.html#readUTF()` — **partial**: Reading the prefixed NeoFlash π 🦀 field yields the same visible string before the tail field. Raw prefix/byte order, malformed UTF and BOM/NUL semantics are not established by the original roundtrip.
- `AS3:method:flash/utils/ByteArray.html#writeUTFBytes()` — **partial**: Writing unprefixed done and reading four bytes back yields the visible suffix. Empty input, malformed surrogates and size boundaries are not positive coverage.
- `AS3:method:flash/utils/ByteArray.html#readUTFBytes()` — **partial**: Reading four bytes after the prefixed Unicode label yields done and exhausts decoded bytes. BOM/NUL, malformed UTF, shorter lengths and overrun behavior are not positive coverage.
- `AS3:method:flash/utils/ByteArray.html#writeBytes()` — **partial**: wire.writeBytes(packet) copies the packet from default source offset 0 into destination offset 3, and later decoding reproduces all fields. Source cursor preservation, overlap and range-clamping semantics are not observed by the original example.
- `AS3:method:flash/utils/ByteArray.html#readBytes()` — **partial**: wire.readBytes(decoded,0,50) transfers the packet while decoded.position remains 7 before decoding from 0. Same-buffer transfers, source-cursor readback, partial copy and bounds failures are not positive coverage.
- `AS3:method:flash/utils/ByteArray.html#clear()` — **partial**: wire.clear() leaves wire.length=0 while decoded packet output remains intact. Cleared position, capacity release and instance accounting are not inspected by the original example.
- `AS3:constant:flash/utils/Endian.html#LITTLE_ENDIAN` — **partial**: The named constant is accepted for packet/decoded byte order in the roundtrip. Raw little-endian payload bytes must be separately observed to validate byte ordering; BIG_ENDIAN is not explicitly referenced.
- `AS3:constructor:flash/display/Sprite.html#Sprite()` — **partial**: new Sprite creates both panels and underlay; project Card extends Sprite and retains identity/state through detach/reparent. General native subclass semantics and authored allocation are not covered.
- `AS3:constructor:flash/display/Shape.html#Shape()` — **partial**: new Shape creates the card badge with independent geometry and an assigned name; its hit can bubble to the card. Other Shape features and native subclassing are not covered.
- `AS3:property:flash/display/Sprite.html#graphics` — **partial**: Runtime Sprite.graphics supplies the typed Graphics facade used for panel/underlay/card solid drawing. Direct Graphics construction, assigning graphics and authored-owner drawing are not covered.
- `AS3:property:flash/display/Shape.html#graphics` — **partial**: Runtime Shape.graphics supplies the badge drawing facade. Direct Graphics construction, assigning graphics and authored-owner drawing are not covered.
- `AS3:method:flash/display/Graphics.html#beginFill()` — **partial**: Solid RGB fills with default opaque alpha render the panels, underlay, card and badge. Other alpha values, gradients/bitmaps and unsupported-owner behavior are not positive coverage.
- `AS3:method:flash/display/Graphics.html#endFill()` — **partial**: The explicit endFill calls complete the original panel/card/badge draw sequences. State transitions across later unfilled/filled commands are not independently discriminated.
- `AS3:method:flash/display/Graphics.html#lineStyle()` — **partial**: A 2-pixel RGB scaling round stroke renders the card and contributes 242x102 bounds around its 240x100 fill. Hairlines, other caps/joins/scale modes and invalid values are not positive coverage.
- `AS3:method:flash/display/Graphics.html#drawRect()` — **partial**: Finite rectangles render the two panels, underlay and 240x100 card, with expected layering and bounds. Negative dimensions, transforms and other path primitives are not covered.
- `AS3:method:flash/display/Graphics.html#drawCircle()` — **partial**: A radius-19 filled circle renders the Shape badge and provides the descendant hit route to the card. Other radii, boundary tessellation fidelity and invalid coordinates are not covered.
- `AS3:method:flash/display/DisplayObjectContainer.html#addChild()` — **partial**: Dynamic panels/Card/badge attach and the same Card reparents while preserving count/listeners. Added leftPanel.addChild(leftPanel) rejects the cycle before changing its valid parent/two children, after which the positive interactive workflow succeeds. Return values, authored-prefix mutation and Flash-native error type/code equivalence are not established.
- `AS3:method:flash/display/DisplayObjectContainer.html#removeChild()` — **partial**: Removing the runtime Card makes parent null and removes it from the image/hit route; adding it again preserves count/listener state. Return value and authored removal are not positive coverage.
- `AS3:method:flash/display/DisplayObjectContainer.html#swapChildren()` — **partial**: The Up cycle swaps Card/underlay and getChildIndex(card) becomes 0 before Card is moved back to the top. Return value and swapping authored children are not exercised.
- `AS3:method:flash/display/DisplayObjectContainer.html#setChildIndex()` — **partial**: After the cycle, setChildIndex(card,numChildren-1) restores the Card above the underlay and permits a subsequent badge/card click. Return value, invalid indices and authored-prefix movement are not positive coverage.
- `AS3:method:flash/display/DisplayObjectContainer.html#getChildIndex()` — **partial**: The original cycle reads Card index 0 immediately after swapping it with underlay. Detached, absent and authored-child index cases are not exercised.
- `AS3:property:flash/display/DisplayObjectContainer.html#numChildren` — **partial**: Startup reports leftPanel=2, rightPanel=0, Card=1; child counts update with reparent/detach and do not count Graphics paints as children. Read-only enforcement and broad container shapes are not covered.
- `as2:MovieClip.html#createEmptyMovieClip()` — **partial**: An empty runtime clip named legacy is created at the append depth and draws an interactive triangle. Arbitrary depth/replacement, linked symbols and historical AVM1 lifecycle are not covered.
- `as2:MovieClip.html#getNextHighestDepth()` — **partial**: The append depth returned for the root is accepted by createEmptyMovieClip. Its numeric value, historical depth gaps and replacement semantics are not inspected.
- `as2:MovieClip.html#lineStyle()` — **partial**: A 2-pixel RGB stroke with legacy 100-percent alpha outlines the triangle. Hairlines, alternate styles and other alpha values are not covered.
- `as2:MovieClip.html#beginFill()` — **partial**: An RGB fill with legacy 100-percent alpha colors the runtime triangle. Other alpha values, gradients/bitmaps and authored-owner drawing are not covered.
- `as2:MovieClip.html#moveTo()` — **partial**: moveTo(0,0) starts the triangle path on the new runtime clip. Other pen-state transitions and unsupported inputs are not covered.
- `as2:MovieClip.html#lineTo()` — **partial**: Three lineTo calls form the closed triangle through (36,30),(0,60),(0,0), including a visible/hittable interior. Other contour/winding behavior is not covered.
- `as2:MovieClip.html#endFill()` — **partial**: endFill follows the closed triangle drawing sequence before pointer interaction. Later fill-state transitions and return values are not inspected.
- `AS3:property:flash/display/DisplayObject.html#parent` — **partial**: Card.parent changes leftPanel -> rightPanel -> null -> leftPanel while the same Card retains count/listeners. General authored reparenting and root parent behavior are not covered.
- `AS3:property:flash/display/DisplayObject.html#root` — **partial**: Card click resolves root.status after reparent/reattach and updates the authored root text. Detached root semantics and cross-root transitions are not exercised.
- `AS3:property:flash/display/DisplayObject.html#name` — **partial**: Runtime names leftPanel/rightPanel/card/badge are assigned and panel names appear after reparent. Duplicate names and name-lookup conflict behavior are not covered.
- `AS3:property:flash/display/DisplayObject.html#x` — **partial**: Explicit x offsets place runtime panels/card/badge, and reparenting preserves the local Card coordinates. Numeric coercion and singular/rotated transforms are not exercised.
- `AS3:property:flash/display/DisplayObject.html#y` — **partial**: Runtime y offsets and Timer-driven Card y increments affect the displayed position; local geometry survives reparent. Numeric coercion and timer scheduling precision are not covered.
- `AS3:method:flash/display/DisplayObject.html#getBounds()` — **partial**: Card.getBounds(root) returns typed Rectangle width242/height102 after a detach/reparent/order cycle, including the 2-pixel stroke. Detached bounds, masks and arbitrary transforms are not exercised.
- `as2:MovieClip.html#_name` — **partial**: New AS2 clip reads legacy in ready/press traces. Renaming and lookup conflicts are not covered.
- `as2:MovieClip.html#_parent` — **partial**: The new AS2 clip reports _parent equal to the root creator. Removal and reparenting through the AS2 facade are not exercised.
- `as2:MovieClip.html#_x` — **partial**: Assigning _x=462 positions the runtime AS2 triangle. Coercion and unusual values are not covered.
- `as2:MovieClip.html#_y` — **partial**: Assigning _y=235 positions the runtime AS2 triangle. Coercion and unusual values are not covered.
- `as2:MovieClip.html#event:onPress` — **partial**: Assigning a function to the runtime AS2 clip onPress produces dynamic AS2 press legacy on its real triangle hit. Other callbacks, pointer buttons and replacement are not covered.
- `AS3:method:flash/events/EventDispatcher.html#addEventListener()` — **partial**: Card MouseEvent.CLICK and Stage keyboard listeners remain effective after dynamic detach/reparent/reattach; the descendant badge click increments the same Card once. Actual ADDED/REMOVED payload/order is not observed by the original fixture.
- `AS3:constant:flash/events/MouseEvent.html#CLICK` — **partial**: The dynamic Card callback receives its body/badge coordinate hit and preserves count through graph mutations. Other mouse event types and engines are not inferred.
- `AS3:constant:flash/events/KeyboardEvent.html#KEY_DOWN` — **partial**: Real Right/Space/Up drive reparent, detach/reattach and reorder through Stage keyboard input. Modifier, IME and repeat behavior are not covered.
- `AS3:method:flash/utils/ByteArray.html#compress()` — **missing**: The added packet.compress() probe raises TypeError: ByteArray.compress is not implemented in the bounded binary-buffer profile, retaining packet length 50, position 50 and first byte 78. Positive numeric/UTF packet decoding still succeeds and repeats after Restart. The diagnostic prevents silent loss; compression itself remains missing.
