### E4X-HTML5-20261027T0608Z — Independent HTML5 acceptance: E4X-HTML5-20261027T0608Z

accepted bounded E4X target lists literals namespaces and content preserving refusal

Only observed E4X selectors, unique-parent target writes, one indexed replacement/deletion, copied literal subtree and qualified metadata path are accepted. Filters, default XML namespace scopes, dynamic names, namespace interpolation, class access namespaces, complete equality/addition and historical ECMA-357 behavior remain unsupported or unverified. The oracle compares ordered expanded names, attributes and logical text. It does not establish lexical XML preservation, original quote/entity/CDATA syntax, all mixed-content whitespace behavior or original Flash parity. Whole-tree before/after refusal equality is exact for the measured three refusals only. Namespace/QName evidence is limited to the invoked two-argument forms and selection behavior; other overloads, properties, equality, mutation and prefix corner cases are not accepted. XMLList literal does not promote its public constructor. Direct XML.setChildren and successful AS2 parseXML were not exercised and retain their prior needs_review state. Corrected handler trace provides narrow XML/XMLList is/as and nullable transfer evidence on this build only. Pretty settings still equal existing defaults; nondefault setter/cross-handler semantics and general reset behavior remain unverified. Previous f4fcb71 handler probe is not retroactively accepted. Literal hole order ATB and resulting content are concrete evidence for this scenario; arbitrary coercion side effects, escaping of every attribute character, exception/reentry ordering, budget/depth limits, comment/PI/CDATA brace permutations and adversarial syntax are not claimed. Pending data loads, stale wrappers, reentrant replacement/owner teardown and all transport races remain unverified. AS2 DOM regression appends a detached clone; it does not test moving an attached node between parents. Only IAB; engine/version not separately identified; original Flash was not run(documented_only). Native Sources/D-008, authoring/import/Undo/clipboard and unsaved-document-dependent work remain pending; the document was untouched. Direct file opening policy refusal was not retried or bypassed. TRANSFORM-AS2-RGB-001 and RESOURCE-FIXTURE-INPUT-001 were not retested or closed. Old handler defaults, AS2 Number conversion and owner-aware guards remain open. Main is already developing Graphics gradients/strokes/cubic curves/roundRect/drawPath/copyFrom; later sources inherit no passes from this E4X snapshot.

- `AS3:constructor:Namespace.html#Namespace()` — **partial**: Two-argument new Namespace("meta", "urn:neoflash:catalog") participates in qualified metadata reads/writes and the equivalent QName lookup. Serialized expanded names retain the metadata URI across revisions 0, 1, 2, Restart, and revision 1. The separate one-argument form and public Namespace accessors/methods are not promoted.
- `AS3:constructor:QName.html#QName():variant:ed6d67a1b434` — **partial**: Two-argument new QName(meta, "note") is used as a computed XML selector. Its result matches catalog.meta::note on original and edited trees in all five observed states. This is the two-argument catalogue variant only; the one-argument QName copy constructor and direct public getters are untested.
- `AS3:constructor:XML.html#XML()` — **partial**: Retains XML-HTML5-20261027T0545Z constructor evidence. This build parses the embedded 367-byte XML source and each edited serialized catalog; visible roundtrip item counts are 3, 4, 4, 3, 4. Complete serialized trees preserve Unicode, logical entity content and the qualified metadata name. Malformed XML and DOCTYPE give caught SyntaxError alongside the positive load; no general XML parser conformance is claimed.
- `AS3:method:XML.html#copy()` — **partial**: Retains the earlier bounded copy evidence from XML-HTML5-20261027T0545Z. Here each revision starts from original.copy(); attribute changes, child creation, item replacement/deletion, fragment insertion and metadata edits affect only the working catalog. All five complete original serializations match the input logical tree. This does not add a post-insertion aliasing probe or prove every copy edge case.

No attached-node moving or new aliasing contract is inferred.
- `AS3:method:XML.html#elements()` — **partial**: Retains XML-HTML5-20261027T0545Z explicit selector evidence. This build directly calls original.elements("item") for the unchanged original count 3 and value.elements("item") across the typed project-to-handler bridge. Namespace wildcard and other selector profiles are not newly exercised.
- `AS3:method:XML.html#length()` — **partial**: The actual first XML element receives a child named length containing "child value". Its property lookup returns that child while first.length() remains 1; the combined sameNameSplit flag is true on all three edits and complete trees include the child. - `AS3:method:XML.html#toXMLString()` — **partial**: Retains XML-HTML5-20261027T0545Z serialization evidence. Ten full AS3 original/working trees, three pre-refusal working trees and three XML literal trees match independent ordered expanded-name/attribute/text expectations. All three before/after ambiguous-edit serializations are exactly equal. XML lexical CDATA/entity/quote spelling and original-player serialization equivalence are not claimed.
- `AS3:method:XMLList.html#length()` — **partial**: Retains previous XMLList length evidence. Actual item selections and descendant-name selections report 3 initially and 4 after each edit; the successfully cast handler XMLList has positive length. This is bounded selected-list counting, not XMLList construction or all live-membership semantics.
- `AS3:method:XMLList.html#toXMLString()` — **partial**: Direct fragment.toXMLString() on the two-item XMLList literal yields PARTS then EXTRA with escaped scalar text; all three serialized fragments match the independent logical tree oracle. - `AS3:operator:operators.html#XML_literal_tag_delimiter` — **partial**: The ordinary project compiles and renders one XML item literal and a two-root XMLList fragment. Three literal trees and three fragment serializations preserve the static apostrophe, escaped ampersand, names, order and injected data. - `AS3:operator:operators.html#braces_(XML)` — **partial**: Whole attribute-value and child-content holes evaluate in the observed ATB order once per edit. ### FILTERS-HTML5-20261027T0844Z — Independent HTML5 acceptance: FILTERS-HTML5-20261027T0844Z

accepted partial filters family

No original Flash runtime or other browser engine. No native filter-authoring UI, filtered DOM text input/IME, full text selection or selectable=true behavior. No actual-browser mask/scrollRect/filter intersections, animated timeline owners, arbitrary reparent/reentry/retirement, ancestor/descendant clip ordering or all nested filter combinations. No high-density raster quality, benchmark/performance or cache/storage/work-budget exhaustion acceptance; implementation limits are documented only. No general coercion/default/property-setter matrix, all filter clones, full invalid-change atomicity or exact Flash kernels/bounds. No remaining filter classes, BitmapData.applyFilter, complete cacheAsBitmap semantics or native save/import filter continuity. Prior AS2 rgb, handler defaults, AS2 Number, owner-aware diagnostics, resource overlay and Native Sources/D-008 remain open or unverified.

- `AS3:constructor:flash/filters/BlurFilter.html#BlurFilter()` — **partial**: Constructed (8,8,1) and (14,3,2) filters render the mixed vector/text/bitmap card as soft and directional blur in the observed logical-pixel IAB profile. Original and getter copies remain detached and clear/restore/Restart recover the corresponding scene. Exact Flash kernels, padding, all quality levels and AVM coercions are not established.
- `AS3:constructor:flash/filters/GlowFilter.html#GlowFilter()` — **partial**: The gallery constructs outer purple and inner cyan glow variants around the complete mixed-content card, with the expected visible phase change and clear/restore behavior. This covers the supplied color/alpha/radius/strength/quality/inner parameters; it does not promote individual sibling properties or exact Flash RGBA semantics.
- `AS3:constructor:flash/filters/DropShadowFilter.html#DropShadowFilter()` — **partial**: The observed card renders the supplied outer shadow in phase 0 and rotated knockout shadow in phase 1; its nested bitmap is filtered before the parent effect. Clear restores original source content and subsequent restore returns the visible shadow profile. No hideObject=true, inner-shadow, every angle/transform or exact padding claim.
- `AS3:constructor:flash/filters/ColorMatrixFilter.html#ColorMatrixFilter()` — **partial**: Supplied grayscale and BRG-swap matrices affect the complete parent card; a separate nested Bitmap uses a BGR swap. Mutating the original constructor array element 0 to 99 leaves the constructed filter and assigned live filter at their original .2126/0 values. All 144 shared source pixels remain unchanged in six observations. No arbitrary 20-coefficient, alpha-offset or historical rounding claim.
- `AS3:property:flash/display/DisplayObject.html#filters` — **partial**: Assign/read/clear of the four cards and nested Bitmap preserve the measured copied values, original card references and listener state within each session. The six states 0/1/2/0/Restart 0/1 show five live filter lists of length 1 outside clear and all 0 when cleared; visuals restore. Assignment of [{}] gives TypeError with old list length 1 and measured blurX intact. No full failure pixel atomicity, null assignment, masks or every DisplayObject receiver claim.
- `AS3:property:flash/filters/BlurFilter.html#blurX` — **partial**: Direct seed mutation to 40 and returned filter-copy mutation to 32 leave assigned live blurX at 8/14. A detached clone mutated to 1 leaves its seed 40. The observed card changes between the corresponding blur profiles. This is bounded valid numeric read/write and copy evidence, not all range/coercion behavior.
- `as2:flash/filters/DropShadowFilter.html#DropShadowFilter()` — **partial**: AS2 constructs the supplied 90-degree shadows at distance 6/12 as the first element of the legacy MovieClip two-filter list. The rendered strip changes with the subsequent glow, clears and restores while its click state persists. No isolated full shadow oracle or individual property getter/setter coverage is inferred.
- `as2:flash/filters/GlowFilter.html#GlowFilter()` — **partial**: AS2 constructs the supplied glow as the second legacy-strip filter, changing blurX 4→9 and outer→inner in the alternate phase. Visible strip effects clear and restore with continued callbacks. This accepts the combined supplied constructor profiles, not individual properties, every order permutation or other AS2 filter classes.
- `as2:MovieClip.html#filters` — **partial**: Assigned the legacy MovieClip ordered shadow/glow list, read its length and cleared with []. Observed phase list counts 2/2/0/2/Restart 2/2 accompany visible clear/restore; clicks 1→2 persist through clear and restart returns the next click to 1. No detached getter-element mutation, other AS2 receiver classes or mask interaction claim.
- `AS3:method:flash/filters/BlurFilter.html#clone()` — **partial**: Explicit seed.clone() returns a different BlurFilter; mutating clone.blurX to 1 leaves seed.blurX 40 in all five filtered-phase observations. This covers the concrete BlurFilter clone and that measured numeric field, not base BitmapFilter.clone or other filter clones.
- `AS3:property:flash/filters/ColorMatrixFilter.html#matrix` — **partial**: Reading cm.matrix returns an array whose element 0 mutation to 77 leaves the original filter and installed copy at .2126/0. Together with constructor-input mutation to 99, this establishes the observed element-zero defensive-copy getter profile. No direct matrix property setter or comprehensive 20-coefficient mutation test was performed.
- `AS3:property:flash/text/TextField.html#selectable` — **partial**: With authored caption.selectable=false, an actual coordinate click at (529, 310) on the glow-card caption reaches the card listener and opens no native text editor; the route repeats after Restart. Root browser observation is recorded separately with matching listener/AX evidence. Positive selectable=true behavior, selection/clipboard and keyboard selection are untested.

### FORMS-HTML5-20261027T0811Z — Independent HTML5 acceptance: FORMS-HTML5-20261027T0811Z

accepted partial forms and bounded typed amf recovery

No original Flash runtime or other browser engine. No real IME composition, complete grapheme/surrogate/caret matrix, password/selectable API acceptance, rich/HTML text, embedded font or historical metrics parity. No general transformed/clipped/occluded native-editor overlays, reentrant focus/listener ownership or all selection ranges. AS2 Selection direct live object only; string paths/levels and full Tab/button navigation remain unaccepted. No broad Error is/as type tests: extra Error is probe rejected during fixture preparation. No EOFError/errorID, broad malformed AMF corpus, storage quotas/denial or atomicity. AS2 rgb, handler defaults, AS2 Number, owner-aware diagnostics, resource overlay, Native Sources/D-008 remain open/unverified.

- `AS3:constant:flash/events/Event.html#CHANGE` — **partial**: Event.CHANGE listener observed committed native edits in both fields. Digit/emoji restriction rejection and cancelled # insertion did not produce changes; programmatic Studio7 replacement also left change counters unchanged. Fresh input after Restart produced one fresh change.
- `AS3:constant:flash/events/FocusEvent.html#FOCUS_IN` — **partial**: FOCUS_IN listener observed real field focus from pointer/Tab and programmatic button routes, including fresh focus after Restart. The trace identifies nickField/noteField and stage.focus equals the target during observed callbacks.
- `AS3:constant:flash/events/FocusEvent.html#FOCUS_OUT` — **partial**: FOCUS_OUT listener observed field-to-field, field-to-button and field-to-null transitions with corresponding old/new names. Full recursive/cancellable focus policies and all browser transitions are not established.
- `AS3:constant:flash/events/TextEvent.html#TEXT_INPUT` — **partial**: TEXT_INPUT listener received actual typed and pasted payloads before committed changes. The # payload was cancelled; digit/emoji payloads reached this listener but were subsequently filtered by restrict. No original-player IME event equivalence is claimed.
- `AS3:constant:flash/text/TextFieldType.html#INPUT` — **partial**: Both created fields compared equal to INPUT and became editable through the real native single-line/multiline controls. DYNAMIC and switching existing types are not covered.
- `AS3:method:flash/text/TextField.html#replaceSelectedText()` — **partial**: Replacing nickname range [0,5) by Studio7 produced Studio7FGHIJKLMNOPQRSTUVWX, UTF-16 length 26, collapsed range/caret 7/7/7. The digit and length beyond maxChars=24 persisted while user counters stayed 18 inputs/15 changes. This is a programmatic replacement bypass of user filters, not arbitrary selection/formatting conformance.
- `AS3:method:flash/text/TextField.html#setSelection()` — **partial**: Programmatically selecting nickname [0,5) then replacing it changed exactly that prefix. Subsequent replacement collapsed selection/caret at 7. This covers the observed valid forward range, not reversed/out-of-bounds/surrogate-boundary policies.
- `AS3:property:flash/display/Stage.html#focus` — **partial**: Setter moved focus to nickField for selection replacement; callback getter comparisons showed stage.focus===event.target for observed focus-ins. Native Tab and AS2 direct-object selection shared this owner. Full authored-button keyboard traversal and focus policies remain outside the accepted profile.
- `AS3:property:flash/events/Event.html#target` — **partial**: Text/focus callback target names and committed text identify the edited nickField or noteField. Native mutations then report the correct receiver text/length/selection. This does not establish all retargeting, bubbling or capture routes.
- `AS3:property:flash/text/TextField.html#caretIndex` — **partial**: Getter observed UTF-16 caret positions during typing and paste, including 24 at the nickname limit, 23 after multiline emoji text, and 7 after programmatic replacement. Arbitrary grapheme/selection-direction conformance is not inferred.
- `AS3:property:flash/text/TextField.html#maxChars` — **partial**: Nickname user insertion of 29 ASCII units became the first 24 permitted letters; programmatic Studio7 replacement and restored text retained length 26. Assigning -1 raised catchable RangeError and readback stayed 24 beside successful editing. Note maxChars=128 was configured but its limit was not reached; all coercions/bounds remain partial.
- `AS3:property:flash/text/TextField.html#restrict` — **partial**: The supplied A-Za-z/А-Яа-я/Ёё/space pattern admitted tested Latin and Cyrillic letters/spaces and rejected individual 7 and emoji insertions without changes. - `AS3:property:flash/text/TextField.html#type` — **partial**: Setting both created fields to TextFieldType.INPUT led to actual editing with text reflected in committed Canvas views and persisted data; getter equality was true at startup/reopen. - `as2:Selection.html#setFocus()` — **partial**: Passing the live noteField returned true, produced corresponding focus callbacks, and allowed selection then real typing. String paths explicitly remain outside this accepted direct-object profile; null and stale-object variants were not newly exercised.

### GEOMETRY-TIMER-HTML5-20261027T0152Z — Independent HTML5 acceptance: GEOMETRY-TIMER-HTML5-20261027T0152Z

accepted bounded geometry timer workflow with documented limits

Only the exact nineteen partial declarations and one needs_review entry receive this evidence; whole geometry/Timer classes and AS2 counterparts are not promoted. Matrix transformPoint uses input y0 and one finite mapping; inverse positive path, arbitrary coefficients, mutation, aliases and other geometry operations remain unverified in-browser. Publication Restart is not public Timer.reset or stop. Delay/repeatCount getters, infinite timers, listener mutation, TimerEvent clone/updateAfterEvent, extreme timing/overflow and background throttling were not tested. Only Codex in-app browser was checked; engine/version not separately identified. Original Flash not run: documented_only. Native Sources/undo/save-reopen/D-008 remain pending; no unsaved native document was touched. Later ByteArray and dynamic display/Graphics work inherits no pass. Aggregate observer exception remains unlocated and separately recorded.

- `AS3:constructor:flash/geom/Matrix.html#Matrix()` — **partial**: Six finite coefficients (140,36,0,1,180,220) construct the mapping used for four observed output positions. Default/invalid arguments and direct slot access are not exercised.
- `AS3:method:flash/geom/Matrix.html#transformPoint()` — **partial**: Transforming Point(tick,0), ticks 1..4, produces (320,256),(460,292),(600,328),(740,364). The zero input y means c/d contributions are not discriminated; arbitrary affine transforms are not established.
- `AS3:constructor:flash/geom/Point.html#Point()` — **partial**: Explicit finite points (tick,0) and (0,0) are passed into matrix/display conversion. Defaults, nonfinite/coercion cases and constructor aliases are not exercised.
- `AS3:property:flash/geom/Point.html#x` — **partial**: Read x of typed Matrix/display conversion results and use it to move/render the marker. Direct point x writes and coercions are not exercised.
- `AS3:property:flash/geom/Point.html#y` — **partial**: Read y of typed Matrix/display conversion results and use it to move/render the marker. Direct point y writes and coercions are not exercised.
- `AS3:property:flash/geom/Rectangle.html#width` — **partial**: Typed display getBounds returns width 32 for the stroked marker at all four positions. Rectangle width writes or direct construction are not exercised.
- `AS3:property:flash/geom/Rectangle.html#height` — **partial**: Typed display getBounds returns height 32 for the stroked marker at all four positions. Rectangle height writes or direct construction are not exercised.
- `AS3:constructor:flash/utils/Timer.html#Timer()` — **partial**: Timer(400,4) drives four typed tick callbacks and completion; Timer(-1,1) raises the caught actionable RangeError before the valid workflow still runs. Other invalid delays/repeat values, default infinite repetition and Flash error-code fidelity were not exercised.
- `AS3:method:flash/utils/Timer.html#start()` — **partial**: Starting a new finite Timer produces ticks 1..4 and one completion; a fresh publication Restart repeats the workflow. Return contract, repeated start and resume-after-stop were not exercised.
- `AS3:property:flash/utils/Timer.html#currentCount` — **partial**: Counter reads progress from 0 to 1,2,3,4; completion reads 4. Each publication Restart initializes a fresh count 0. Read-only enforcement and overflow were not exercised.
- `AS3:property:flash/utils/Timer.html#running` — **partial**: The finite timer completion callback reads running=false. No direct running=true getter observation or invalid write was made.
- `AS3:constant:flash/events/TimerEvent.html#TIMER` — **partial**: The TIMER constant registers the typed tick callback used for four coordinate updates. No other TimerEvent constant or member is inferred.
- `AS3:constant:flash/events/TimerEvent.html#TIMER_COMPLETE` — **partial**: The TIMER_COMPLETE constant registers one completion callback after the fourth tick. No other TimerEvent member is inferred.
- `AS3:event:flash/utils/Timer.html#event:timer` — **partial**: Four typed tick callbacks occur in order before completion and update rendered marker/text. Listener mutation, event-target fields and timing edge cases are not exercised.
- `AS3:event:flash/utils/Timer.html#event:timerComplete` — **partial**: One typed completion callback follows tick 4 and observes count 4/running=false in each completed run. Alternative repeat counts and listener mutation were not exercised.
- `AS3:function:flash/utils/package.html#getTimer()` — **partial**: Imported getTimer produces visible elapsed 1617 ms at first completion and 1612 ms after publication Restart, despite the intervening wall time. This supports elapsed-session reporting/rebasing; exact tick timing, overflow and original Flash scheduling are not asserted.
- `AS3:method:flash/display/DisplayObject.html#getBounds()` — **partial**: Result assigns to typed Rectangle and reports 32x32, preserving the shared geometry constructor identity in this workflow.
- `AS3:method:flash/display/DisplayObject.html#localToGlobal()` — **partial**: Result assigns to typed Point and agrees with marker translation in the untransformed root; nested rotation/scale are not covered.
- `AS3:method:flash/events/EventDispatcher.html#addEventListener()` — **partial**: Timer listeners register typed methods and receive the expected four tick/one completion sequence; broader dispatcher methods are not inferred.
- `AS3:method:flash/geom/Matrix.html#invert()` — **needs_review**: The pinned geom.js contains a finite Matrix inverse implementation, and the browser confirms an actionable RangeError for a singular zero matrix without preventing subsequent valid transform/timer work. Successful non-singular inversion was not independently exercised; this is needs_review, not accepted implementation support.

### GRAPHICS-HTML5-20261027T0619Z — Independent HTML5 acceptance: GRAPHICS-HTML5-20261027T0619Z

accepted bounded gradient graphics paths and copied drawing with live instances

Only opaque gradient stops were used. Partial alpha, invisible-gradient hit testing, exact stroke-edge hits, focal endpoints, every stop/coercion rule, unsupported spread/interpolation and allocation/depth/command budgets remain unverified. REFLECT refusal does not implement reflection. NON_ZERO constant is exercised on a simple contour only; overlap/winding discrimination, wide/quadratic commands and other path modes are not accepted. drawRoundRectComplex is an observed NeoFlash convenience extension, not an Adobe declaration and not counted in the AS catalog. Gradient copy evidence covers a source redraw with a stable copied destination. It does not establish every source/destination release, arbitrary input arrays/Matrix aliases or stale-facade/reentrant owner behavior. Restart is an active scene reset, not a pending allocation cancellation test. No exact historical rasterization or original Flash run. Pixel comparisons establish the fixed screenshots of this IAB profile only. Engine/version not separately identified; no other browser accepted. Dynamic card has no semantic action entry, so no separate accessibility or keyboard-navigation pass is claimed; Right/Space/Up stage key controls did work. Native gradient tools/authoring/import/Undo and Native Sources/D-008 remain pending; unsaved native document untouched. AS2rgb, resource overlay, old handler defaults, AS2 Number and owner-aware guards were not retested or closed. File URL policy refusal was not retried or bypassed. Main is already developing masks/scrollRect. Later live sources receive no passes from this frozen08f1d9c batch. Earlier independently accepted sources and baseline remain separately identified.

- `AS3:method:flash/display/Graphics.html#beginGradientFill()` — **partial**: The saved initial/click 1/cycle screenshots show a linear card palette changing on real clicks, a radial badge and the retained panel wave; the copy keeps its earlier palette. Linear and radial native-Matrix gradients with opaque stops, pad spreading and RGB interpolation. Includes a radial focal ratio -0.35. REFLECT refusal is not support; general gradient alpha, repeat, reflect, linearRGB and historical raster parity are outside this example.
- `AS3:method:flash/display/Graphics.html#lineGradientStyle()` — **partial**: The card and scaled copied drawing both have visible gradient outlines in the reviewed initial/click 1 screenshots. Native-Matrix two-stop linear gradient stroke on the rounded card; no general stroke caps/joins/scale modes or allocation-limit acceptance.
- `AS3:method:flash/display/Graphics.html#drawRoundRect()` — **partial**: The rounded card is visible, then the actual click sequence is 1 leftPanel, 2 rightPanel, 3 leftPanel, Restart and fresh 1 leftPanel; reported drawing dimensions remain 242 by 102 including stroke. drawRoundRect(0,0,240,100,28,28) with existing fill/stroke; no every-radius, negative-size or unequal ellipse edge case. drawRoundRectComplex is a separate NeoFlash extension and not an Adobe Graphics declaration.
- `AS3:method:flash/display/Graphics.html#cubicCurveTo()` — **partial**: The light curved stroke is present across the right-panel wave in the reviewed initial and click 1 images. Direct cubicCurveTo(100,75,245,220,366,135) after moveTo(12,155); rendered scene evidence, not a new per-curve analytic bounds or pixel-equivalence test.
- `AS3:method:flash/display/Graphics.html#drawPath()` — **partial**: The intended wave is visible and preserved throughout the family; caught malformed-data RangeError and unchanged panel bounds 520/190/380/190 are recorded twice. MOVE_TO/LINE_TO/CUBIC_CURVE_TO command Vector and numeric data render a simple nonzero path. Malformed [MOVE_TO,LINE_TO] with data [0,0,2] refuses; reported bounds are unchanged. Without a pre-refusal screenshot or full graph snapshot this is bounded refusal/content evidence, not complete atomicity proof.
- `AS3:method:flash/display/Graphics.html#copyFrom()` — **partial**: Saved image comparison reports all 11,200 pixels of the fixed copiedPaint crop unchanged after click 1, click 3 and detach, while all 11,625 pixels of the selected live-card interior change. Copying card.graphics into a Shape preserves fill/stroke drawing in a visibly separate scaled object. Later source clear/redraw changes its palette while the copy stays unchanged; other source/destination lifetime and mutation profiles are not inferred.
- `AS3:method:flash/display/Graphics.html#clear()` — **partial**: The live card changes palette on each click; source clear/redraw does not alter the fixed copiedPaint crop. Restart restores the initial paint and the next click records 1 once. clear followed by redraw of the same rounded geometry updates the current palette. No claim that all previous possible paths or stale facade cases were independently checked.
- `AS3:method:flash/display/Graphics.html#moveTo()` — **partial**: The direct moveTo creates the starting point of the retained visible light cubic stroke; this is separate from the Vector MOVE_TO command. - `AS3:method:flash/geom/Matrix.html#createGradientBox()` — **partial**: The image retains varied linear/radial gradients despite the trace confirming source matrix tx 900 after paint, at initial and every repaint. Later card source box.tx=900 does not shift the retained original drawing; no broad Matrix or all rotation conformance is inferred.
- `as2:MovieClip.html#beginGradientFill()` — **partial**: The legacy gradient triangle remains visible and a real press produces exactly one "dynamic AS2 press legacy" trace. AS2 linear native-Matrix gradient with percentage alpha [100,100] reaches an visible triangle. Only input alpha 100 is exercised, not partial or transparent AS2 opacity conversion or legacy object-matrix formats.
- `as2:MovieClip.html#lineGradientStyle()` — **partial**: The legacy gradient triangle remains visible and a real press produces exactly one "dynamic AS2 press legacy" trace. - `AS3:constant:flash/display/GraphicsPathCommand.html#CUBIC_CURVE_TO` — **partial**: The named constant is supplied to the actual successfully rendered operation in this family. ### HTML5-ROTATION-RANGE — HTML5-ROTATION-RANGE

rotation/_rotation=370 reads back 370. AS3 Adobe documentation specifies normalization into [-180,180], hence 10. Visual angle alone can hide this semantic difference.

Observed evidence: confirmed_internal_against_AS3_documentation. This is not an original Flash differential result.

Evidence: [crates/neoflash-core/src/runtime.rs](../../crates/neoflash-core/src/runtime.rs)

### IMAGE-PIPELINE-HTML5-20261027T0943Z — Independent HTML5 acceptance: IMAGE-PIPELINE-HTML5-20261027T0943Z

accepted partial image pipeline family

IAB only; engine/version not separately identified; original Flash documented_only. Complete PNG and source/tint arithmetic oracle applies to initial state only. Later screenshot/AX evidence cannot recover missing trace types, owners or errors. No complete Flash filter kernel/raster/premultiplication/rounding/HiDPI conformance, opaque-destination/self-alias behavior or all defaults/coercions. No detached capture, mask/scroll/filter intersections, clipRect/colorTransform/blend variants, display text/vector draw, smoothing options or general reentry/retirement lifecycle acceptance. No JPEG/JPEG-XR output, existing output-buffer encode overload, false/default compression setter behavior, quota/budget/depth stress, full raw-endian independent oracle or ByteArray compression. AS2 processing is a bounded source-language/shared-AS3-created-receiver path. AS2 ColorTransform.rgb is a separate still-open gap. No repeated late refusal/owner/PNG traces, full-run zeroerror or unobserved engine claims. Old handler defaults, AS2 Number, owner-aware diagnostics, resource fixture overlay, native Sources/D-008 remain open or unverified; native authoring/document untouched.

- `AS3:method:flash/display/BitmapData.html#colorTransform()` — **partial**: Initial real HTML5 phase 0 applies (.85,1,1,1,12,0,0,0) to a cloned 96x64 image. All 6144 independently decoded tinted PNG pixels match the finite-channel arithmetic profile, while all source pixels retain the authored five-rectangle oracle. Invalid offset 256 is caught as Error and the full source remains intact. Later phases visibly change and restore in saved screenshots, but their exact PNG streams were not captured. ### LINKED-INTERFACES-HTML5-20261027T0243Z — Independent HTML5 acceptance: LINKED-INTERFACES-HTML5-20261027T0243Z

accepted bounded positive linked and interface workflows with documented frame stopping AS2 host error boundary

The aggregate shows4ready/1runtime_error because the deliberate unknown-link fixture aborts; the separately exported original positive control passes. Do not report five clean aggregate cases. Only explicit linkage, append depth and one data-only initObject were exercised. Depth replacement, arbitrary initObject coercion, reentrant construction/lifecycle and authored-owner replacement remain outside this pass. Startup records left first twice. The transcript is preserved without claiming complete Flash frame-action scheduling equivalence. Interface parents and signatures in these sources are accepted, but uncalled reset/accept/post/write-only/static-setter methods and negative compile-signature cases receive no browser pass. Publication Restart is not original Flash differential evidence. Only Codex in-app browser was used; no other engine pass claimed. The native unsaved document was untouched. Clipboard closure has main test/source evidence only; native Sources/D-008 remains pending. Later resource/loader changes do not inherit this frozen build evidence.

- `AS3:statement:statements.html#interface` — **partial**: Project interfaces, including two-parent ICounter, supply typed model slots and property/method dispatch. ICounter(model) preserves identity; ICounter({value:3}) raises the caught TypeError AS3 app.ICounter value required. Full interface argument/return/static type checking and is/as operators were not exercised.
- `AS3:statement:statements.html#implements` — **partial**: Counter implements ICounter and BoundedCounter inherits that nominal contract. Both models enter interface-typed slots; valid function-style interface conversion preserves identity and an unrelated lookalike is rejected. The original unused reset/accept members are not accepted by this run.
- `AS3:statement:statements.html#extends` — **partial**: ICounter extends two project interfaces and BoundedCounter extends Counter; inherited getter and overriding setter coexist on independent model instances. Native-interface and broader inheritance conformance are not covered.
- `AS3:statement:statements.html#get` — **partial**: Instance value/label getters and static Counter.created getter produce bounded:2..9, other:7 and created 2. A write to getter-only label raises a caught read-only TypeError. Getter invocation counts, write-only access and static setter behavior were not tested.
- `AS3:statement:statements.html#set` — **partial**: Constructor initialization and eight model.increment calls write through the inherited/overridden int value setter: model 2 advances 3..9 then remains 9, while other 7 is unchanged. Restart returns 2/7 and next click 3/7. Coercion-hook counts, command/static setters and assignment-expression results were not tested.
- `AS3:statement:statements.html#override` — **partial**: BoundedCounter overrides value setter while retaining inherited getter, and overrides label getter. Eight real clicks demonstrate upper clamp 9 and independent sibling 7; Restart restores state. Other accessor-half combinations and lower-bound coercions were not tested.

### LOADER-HTML5-20261027T0709Z — Independent HTML5 acceptance: LOADER-HTML5-20261027T0709Z

accepted bounded png jpeg loading and owned display lifecycle

No SWF/GIF decoding, application/security domains, native Loader subclasses, general legacy target paths/levels, complete garbage collection or cross-origin CORS acceptance. Both held responses arrive after Restart. Pending close alone is not isolated; only immediate embedded close, combined close→Restart and direct pending Restart suppression are observed. Arbitrary callback-triggered replacement/reentry, pending-load preservation of committed content, owner replacement and allocation quotas remain unverified. Only the previously detached initial PNG is retained and re-adopted. Current JPEG alias lifetime across unload, full disposal and every old LoaderInfo relationship are not established. The fixture does not directly observe progress/open/httpStatus dispatch, AS2 start/progress/error listeners, listener removal, managed-child mutation refusal, unloadAndStop or general coercion. These declarations are not promoted by source implementation alone. No original Flash or other browser engine was run. Native pixel samples are compared with independently decoded fixture data; screenshots establish this IAB display profile rather than exact Flash rasterization or smoothing/alpha-hit semantics. AS2rgb, resource overlay, native Sources/D-008, handler defaults, AS2 Number and full owner-aware guards remain open. Unsaved native document untouched; no file URL retry. EVID-001 build-stage correction belongs to the prior clipping record. Current main work is SharedObject and ByteArray AMF persistence. Later live sources inherit no pass from this frozen039afe6 delivery. Initial dashboard request used an incorrect /published prefix and returned HTTP404 before batch startup; correcting to the server root was harness navigation setup, not a runtime or exporter failure.

- `AS3:constructor:flash/display/Loader.html#Loader()` — **partial**: Actual zero-argument Loader objects accept image content into the display graph and route clicks through their interactive container. Ordinary source publication shows both URL and byte-loaded images, with one managed child at INIT. No native subclassing or class-wide Loader support is claimed.
- `AS3:method:flash/display/Loader.html#load()` — **partial**: Ordinary HTML5 loads the embedded 411-byte PNG and then a real same-origin HTTP 3174-byte JPEG into the same Loader. Both are 96 by 64; measured samples match the independent resource decoder oracle, and visible images swap/restore. Real HTTP requests and successful responses are recorded. No SWF/GIF, LoaderContext, cross-origin/security-domain or complete load-transition timing support is inferred.
- `AS3:method:flash/display/Loader.html#loadBytes()` — **partial**: The original ByteArray contains 413 or 411 PNG bytes, is passed to loadBytes and immediately cleared to length 0. The later byte-loaded image still renders the expected opposite PNG and sample, establishing the snapshot in this asynchronous profile. Invalid non-image bytes produce a visible IOErrorEvent beside working images. Other formats, streaming and input-budget limits are not covered.
- `AS3:method:flash/display/Loader.html#close()` — **partial**: Immediate embedded-load close is observed with no completion and a zero cancelled-callback counter. A separate real HTTP request is received before close is called, but both held responses arrive only after Restart. Therefore the late-response evidence establishes the combined close-then-Restart path for hold-close and Restart suppression for hold-restart; it does not isolate pending HTTP close without Restart. Keep this API partial for the immediate cancellation profile; independent pending-close behavior still needs review. No decode-stage cancellation, reentrant callback or all event-phase contract is inferred.
- `AS3:method:flash/display/Loader.html#unload()` — **partial**: Actual unload yields picture.content==null, picture.numChildren==0 and bytesPicture.content==null, and emits one observed UNLOAD event on the listened-to LoaderInfo. The retained initial PNG Bitmap/BitmapData had already detached during JPEG replacement; it remains readable across phase 2 unload and is re-adopted at its original visual position with an identical 30,602-pixel crop. Later restoration/restart re-create visible images. The current JPEG content was not separately retained, so its alias survival after unload is not established. No unloadAndStop, full garbage collection or general retention-budget behavior is claimed.
- `AS3:property:flash/display/Loader.html#content` — **partial**: At INIT the loaded content is the single managed child and its parent is picture. Replacement changes that content while the explicitly retained first Bitmap becomes detached and preserves its pixel sample. unload returns null and zero children; the retained Bitmap can be independently re-adopted. No direct mouse-listener behavior is accepted for Bitmap itself.
- `AS3:property:flash/display/Loader.html#contentLoaderInfo` — **partial**: The obtained facade receives actual INIT/COMPLETE/IO_ERROR/UNLOAD events and exposes measured image metadata. At each observed INIT its content and loader identities match the managed Loader and Bitmap. This does not implement a public LoaderInfo constructor, arbitrary dispatch or application/security-domain APIs.
- `AS3:property:flash/display/LoaderInfo.html#width` — **partial**: Observed initialized PNG and real HTTP JPEG both report width 96. This is metadata for the measured resources, not arbitrary image-size limits or pre-INIT behavior.
- `AS3:property:flash/display/LoaderInfo.html#height` — **partial**: Observed initialized PNG and real HTTP JPEG both report height 64. No dimension-budget stress or all decoder formats are accepted.
- `AS3:property:flash/display/LoaderInfo.html#bytesLoaded` — **partial**: Completed embedded PNG counts 411/413 and HTTP JPEG count 3174 match exact resource lengths. The initial unloaded Loader reports 0. Intermediate progress-event behavior is not inferred.
- `AS3:property:flash/display/LoaderInfo.html#bytesTotal` — **partial**: Direct completed reads match bytesLoaded for 411/413-byte PNGs and the 3174-byte JPEG. Streaming, missing Content-Length and partial progress counts remain untested.
- `AS3:property:flash/display/LoaderInfo.html#contentType` — **partial**: Direct initialized metadata reports image/png for embedded/byte-loaded PNGs and image/jpeg for the real HTTP JPEG. Unsupported bytes cause an error instead of a successful MIME type.
- `AS3:property:flash/display/LoaderInfo.html#url` — **partial**: Direct metadata reports images/first.png for the embedded request, /independent/second.jpg for the HTTP JPEG, and the empty string for loadBytes. This accepts the measured request-form metadata, not normalization of arbitrary relative or redirected URLs.
- `AS3:property:flash/display/LoaderInfo.html#loaderURL` — **partial**: The property reports the active ordinary publication index.html URL for both embedded and HTTP-loaded content in this run. Cross-domain, redirection and alternate embedding cases are not claimed.
- `AS3:property:flash/display/LoaderInfo.html#content` — **partial**: At each of four observed INIT deliveries, LoaderInfo.content===picture.content is true, with the Bitmap present as a managed child. This is canonical content identity in this loaded-image profile only.
- `AS3:property:flash/display/LoaderInfo.html#loader` — **partial**: At each observed INIT, LoaderInfo.loader===picture is true. Other LoaderInfo ownership, unload/retired access and domain cases remain separate.
- `AS3:event:flash/display/LoaderInfo.html#event:init` — **partial**: Actual traces show initialized content/loader/parent identities before each COMPLETE. The first session order advances IC, ICIC, ICICIC, then Restart produces fresh IC. No exhaustive Flash event timing or reentrant event-callback contract is inferred.
- `AS3:event:flash/display/LoaderInfo.html#event:complete` — **partial**: Successful embedded/HTTP URL and byte-loaded image completions update pixels and metadata once per accepted operation. URL and byte counts progress 1/2/3 and reset to 1. Held old HTTP responses arrive after Restart and produce no stale COMPLETE; this establishes reset suppression and the combined close-then-Restart path, not pending close alone.
- `AS3:event:flash/display/LoaderInfo.html#event:ioError` — **partial**: Actual invalid ByteArray image data invokes IOErrorEvent and exposes the diagnostic that only complete PNG/JPEG are supported, while positive images still display. This is safe refusal, not implementation of SWF/GIF loading, decode-error recovery on an already committed target or native historical error equivalence.
- `AS3:event:flash/display/LoaderInfo.html#event:unload` — **partial**: One observed picture.contentLoaderInfo UNLOAD listener fires during explicit phase 2 unload, followed by null content and zero children. Implicit replacement, repeat unload of an empty loader and all historic event ordering are not accepted from this single profile.
- `AS3:constant:flash/events/Event.html#INIT` — **partial**: Event.INIT selects the actual LoaderInfo initialization listener; initialized content identity is observed before COMPLETE. Other Event constants are not promoted from this row.
- `AS3:constant:flash/events/Event.html#UNLOAD` — **partial**: Event.UNLOAD selects the actual LoaderInfo unload listener, observed once on explicit unload. The enum and this event route are bounded to the measured image lifecycle.
- `AS3:property:flash/display/DisplayObject.html#loaderInfo` — **partial**: The loaded Bitmap directly reports content.loaderInfo===picture.contentLoaderInfo at each of four INIT deliveries. This accepts canonical association for managed loaded image content only, not root/authored/unloaded/retired DisplayObject cases.
- `as2:MovieClipLoader.html#MovieClipLoader()` — **partial**: Actual native zero-argument MovieClipLoader replaces an authored non-root MovieClip target with PNG/JPEG content and drives legacy listener callbacks. It is a bounded image loader, not full SWF/AVM1 or native subclassing support.
- `as2:MovieClipLoader.html#addListener()` — **partial**: The registered ordinary object receives onLoadComplete and onLoadInit across load/swap/restore and Restart. Only registration and actual delivery are accepted; Boolean return, duplicate registration, removal and listener-reentrancy behavior are untested.
- `as2:MovieClipLoader.html#loadClip()` — **partial**: Direct live MovieClip target is replaced by the embedded PNG and real same-origin HTTP JPEG; onLoadComplete reports target===legacyTarget and source images visibly swap. Original placeholder content does not return after unload. String paths, numeric levels, SWF/GIF and the loadClip return value are untested.
- `as2:MovieClipLoader.html#unloadClip()` — **partial**: Phase2 unload removes the loaded legacy image and leaves the target empty instead of restoring its authored placeholder. Clicking the emptied region yields no new legacy callback. A later direct-target load restores the PNG. The method return value and arbitrary/unloaded target cases are not claimed.
- `as2:MovieClipLoader.html#getProgress()` — **partial**: Actual onLoadInit reads both bytesLoaded and bytesTotal as 411 for embedded PNG and 3174 for HTTP JPEG. Counts match independent resource lengths and server delivery. Intermediate progress, targets with no operation and progress-event callbacks are not promoted.
- `as2:MovieClipLoader.html#event:onLoadComplete` — **partial**: Observed legacy order is CI, CICI, CICICI, then fresh CI after Restart. The callback receives the original target identity and status 0 for embedded PNG or 200 for actual HTTP JPEG. This bounded complete-before-init behavior is not a full historical player lifecycle comparison.
- `as2:MovieClipLoader.html#event:onLoadInit` — **partial**: Initialization appends I after each C, updates actual getProgress values, and installs the target onPress callback. Real loaded-target clicks yield legacy counts 1 then 2; the empty target produces no callback. Complete legacy initialization, old authored-script retirement and all reentrant cases are not inferred.

selected parseInt and toFixed regressions pass Number conversion still open

documented_only; no original Flash runtime or SWF-version matrix. Selected parseInt conversion/whitespace/rounding/arity boundaries remain; historical toFixed rounding, boxed values and method closures are not complete. Static screenshot labels are illustrative; actual authored trace is the acceptance evidence. Slots/defaults, rotation and public return contracts are not covered.

### P0-VISIBILITY-20261026T2353Z — Independent HTML5 acceptance: P0-VISIBILITY-20261026T2353Z

passes in verified boolean visibility scope

Boolean true/false only is accepted for AS2/AS3 authored MovieClip placements. Non-Boolean AS2 conversions have not been established against a Flash runtime. TextField and native Button visibility were not individually browser-tested; their shared proxy route is source-reviewed only. Root hiding, stale handles, symbol replacement and overlap click-through are supplemental Rust/Node checks, not additional browser acceptance claims. TextField.text, width and AS2 onEnterFrame assignment still fall through to custom fields in this snapshot; they remain open P0. XFL policy/identity and intrinsic changes present in this frozen snapshot were not independently accepted in this run.

- `as2:MovieClip.html#_visible` — **implemented_subset**: Boolean true/false on authored MovieClip placements: pixels, pointer/semantic targets, hidden clocks/Load and preserved state. Other conversions and original Flash differential unverified.
- `AS3:property:flash/display/DisplayObject.html#visible` — **implemented_subset**: Boolean true/false on authored MovieClip placements and child-local inherited visibility; does not certify every DisplayObject subclass or constructor.
- `as2:TextField.html#_visible` — **needs_review**: New shared visibility route makes the historical absence inference stale; dedicated TextField browser fixture still required.
- `as2:Button.html#_visible` — **needs_review**: New shared visibility route exists; native Button behavior/state and visibility need a dedicated browser fixture.

accepted bounded portable resource and AS2 AS3 HTTP workflow

Only the exact members and workflows observed receive partial status; no whole-class support is inferred. Direct file:// opening remains pending after the main browser protocol-policy denial; no bypass/retry attempted. Embedded resolution under HTTP is not a direct-file browser pass. Cross-origin CORS, redirects, streamed/unknown-length responses, all errorIDs/coercions and complete original Flash event ordering remain unverified. Only form POST bodies and permitted custom headers were exercised; binary/text outgoing bodies are not accepted by this run. An initial verification-fixture extension called the AS2 helper as an undeclared AS3 function; compiler rejected it before browser work. The helper was invoked through an authored AS2 Key listener instead. Failed preparation and its logs remain separate from the four-case measured batch. Earlier attempted clicks on the text overlay issued no GET; only the observed bare rectangle GET is accepted. Native Library/Sources/D-008 and source-resource Undo/clipboard are pending; user unsaved document untouched. No original Flash; documented_only and only IAB. No general AMF interoperability, typed/class aliases/externalizable/XML/Vector/Dictionary/native/display serialization, AMF0 ByteArray support, full graph bounds/quotas or AMF3 trait grammar accepted. AS3 typed catch failure remains open; native-write refusal preserves observed fields only, not general bytewise atomicity. No actual browser storage denial/quota, minDiskSpace reservation, remote SharedObject, .sol files or permission dialogs accepted. No defaultObjectEncoding setter, SharedObject objectEncoding setter, explicit localPath, HTTPS secure behavior, cross-origin/tab synchronization, pagehide durability, stale facade or unflushed-change lifetime accepted. AS2rgb/resource overlay/Native SourcesD-008/defaults/Number/owner-aware gaps not rechecked. Unsaved native document untouched. Later TextField forms development inherits no passes from eddcc30. Tabs51/52 closed and server8768 stopped. Same source tree remained unchanged by independent verification; test fixture/artifact files are ignored target outputs.

- `AS3:property:flash/net/SharedObject.html#data` — **partial**: Authored score 25/50, shared hero aliases, root self-cycle, UTC Date, inventory, undefined/NaN/Infinity/negative-zero values and nested ByteArray badge survived explicit flush and re-opening. Browser traces show LEVEL-1/LEVEL-2 from the reopened badge. This is a bounded ordinary-record graph, not arbitrary native/class serialization.
- `AS3:method:flash/net/SharedObject.html#flush()` — **partial**: Synchronous explicit flush returned flushed and persisted two observed ordinary-record states across Reload/Restart. This is a codec refusal observation, not storage-quota, permission, pending-dialog or full persistence atomicity acceptance.
- `AS3:method:flash/net/SharedObject.html#clear()` — **partial**: Clearing score 50 reset the visible saved state; the new data object differed from a retained prior object whose score remained 50. Reopening showed an empty store. The observed AS3 data-object replacement is distinct from the AS2 in-place clear profile.
- `AS3:property:flash/net/SharedObject.html#objectEncoding` — **partial**: Getter returned default AMF3 value 3 on the observed AS3 store before and after reopening. No SharedObject objectEncoding setter, migration or static defaultObjectEncoding profile was exercised.
- `AS3:property:flash/net/SharedObject.html#size` — **partial**: The observed saved record reported size 179 for both score states; the getter returned the same value after an unsupported native value was removed following refused flush. Unsupported display/native serialization gave TypeError with length and position unchanged; no byte-for-byte failure atomicity or full AMF profile is claimed.
- `AS3:method:flash/utils/ByteArray.html#readObject()` — **partial**: Read one value from the observed AMF3/AMF0 graph streams, preserving tested values/references and leaving tail3/tail0 available; independent wire parsing consumes 144/176 bytes before the 7-byte tails. - `AS3:constant:flash/net/SharedObjectFlushStatus.html#FLUSHED` — **partial**: Both observed successful explicit AS3 flush results compared equal to SharedObjectFlushStatus.FLUSHED and logged flushed. PENDING and asynchronous permission outcomes were not exercised.
- `as2:SharedObject.html#getLocal()` — **partial**: Same-name AS2 facade identity, empty initial store, explicit save/reopen and separate publication-path isolation were observed alongside AS3. Optional localPath, secure and remote/shared connection profiles were not exercised.

### TEXT-LAYOUT-HTML5-20261027T0409Z — Independent HTML5 acceptance: TEXT-LAYOUT-HTML5-20261027T0409Z

accepted bounded dynamic text uniform formats and shared layout

Uniform whole-field formats only. HTML/CSS, mixed character runs, text input/selection/IME, justified alignment, autoSize, advanced paragraph formatting and font embedding remain outside the supported profile. Refusal is not implementation. Only observed declarations receive bounded overrides. AS2 counterparts not invoked, native TextField subclasses, other dimensions/characters/fonts, rotated clipping and all type/coercion errors are not accepted by this run. Actual installed Arial/fallback measurements produce this line count and metric; fonts are not embedded and original Flash rasterization or cross-engine equivalence was not tested. Only T keyboard action updates programmatically generated text; this does not establish user-editable input text support. Native interactive Text tool/Properties/conversion/Undo and Sources/D-008 remain pending. The user unsaved document was not touched; the earlier direct-file URL policy refusal was not retried. The main chat has already started Sound/SoundChannel/SoundTransform/AS2 Sound. Later audio source edits inherit no result from this frozen text build.

- `AS3:constructor:flash/text/TextField.html#TextField()` — **partial**: Two new TextField instances use independent text/format/box state in the common scene graph. Default100×100 state, subclassing and all initialization contracts are unverified.
- `AS3:constructor:flash/text/TextFormat.html#TextFormat()` — **partial**: TextFormat with Arial/20/RGB initial arguments and an empty patch format produce the visible uniform fields. Unsupported constructor fields, coercion and complete historical defaults are unverified.
- `AS3:method:flash/text/TextField.html#appendText()` — **partial**: Appending a newline and text succeeds on the initial uniform primary field. Appending MIXED to the fixed field after its current/default formats differ raises the explicit mixed-format diagnostic and preserves the full text equality; the primary cycle remains usable. Mixed runs are not implemented, and full Unicode boundary behavior is not claimed.
- `AS3:method:flash/text/TextField.html#replaceText()` — **partial**: Replacing UTF-16 range0..6 changes Draft: to Ready: in actual displayed text. Surrogate boundaries, deletion and inserts under different formats are not covered.
- `AS3:method:flash/text/TextField.html#getTextFormat()` — **partial**: Whole-field reads expose current alignment and fixed color/bold/italic. Mutating a returned format copy to red leaves the actual fixed color10802421 unchanged. The partial-range companion is rejected; mixed-run range reads remain unverified.
- `AS3:method:flash/text/TextField.html#setTextFormat()` — **partial**: Whole-field patches visibly apply fixed color/bold/italic and primary center/right alignment. setTextFormat(redCopy,0,1) raises a caught whole-field-range diagnostic and primary color remains15661055. Mixed character runs and every overload are not accepted.
- `AS3:property:flash/text/TextField.html#text` — **partial**: Full setting, prefix replacement and append produce the visible primary text. Whole replacement of fixed text reapplies its insertion defaults without affecting primary width/alignment. Restart restores both original texts/styles; implicit nonstring conversion, rich/input text and all Unicode edge cases remain outside scope.
- `AS3:property:flash/text/TextField.html#defaultTextFormat` — **partial**: Current fixed format is color10802421/bold true/italic true while insertion defaults remain15661055/false/false. Whole-text replacement via T switches actual fixed pixels and current getters to the regular white defaults; primary stays width300/right. Mutation of a default-format getter copy was not tested.
- `AS3:property:flash/text/TextField.html#multiline` — **partial**: Explicit true produces multiline content in both AS3 boxes with a newline and wrapping. False/toggle behavior and all newline forms are unverified.
- `AS3:property:flash/text/TextField.html#wordWrap` — **partial**: Explicit true causes the changing field to reflow across340,220,300 logical widths while its fixed sibling retains width/content. Nonwrapping and long-token boundary behavior are unverified.
- `AS3:property:flash/text/TextField.html#numLines` — **partial**: On the actual browser font profile, the primary shows2 lines at340/left,4 at220/center and3 at300/right; scaleX stays1. Restart restores2 and the next click gives4 once. Counts are specific to the observed font/environment, not historical Flash parity.
- `AS3:property:flash/display/DisplayObject.html#width` — **partial**: On TextField only,340→220→300 logical width reflows the primary without changing scaleX1; fixed stays340. After T replaces fixed text, primary remains300/right. Width behavior under non-unit scale/rotation and other display owners is not inferred.
- `AS3:property:flash/display/DisplayObject.html#scaleX` — **partial**: The TextField status reads scaleX1 while width changes trigger layout rather than glyph scaling. Scale mutation and other owners are not accepted by this read.
- `AS3:property:flash/text/TextFormat.html#align` — **partial**: The actual primary renders left initially, then center at220 and right at300; current alignment getters agree and the fixed field remains independent. Restart restores left. Justify and advanced paragraph behavior remain absent.
- `AS3:property:flash/text/TextFormat.html#leading` — **partial**: Setting leading6 in the initial AS3 uniform format affects the multiline field profile. Precise font-dependent line-spacing parity, negative leading and later changes are not established.
- `AS3:property:flash/text/TextFormat.html#color` — **partial**: The fixed current format reads10802421(0xa4d4f5) and renders blue, while its default reads15661055(0xeef7ff). A returned copy mutation leaves current color unchanged; whole text replacement renders/reads the white default. Per-run colors and invalid/coerced values are unverified.
- `AS3:property:flash/text/TextFormat.html#bold` — **partial**: Fixed current bold reads true and renders bold while default bold remains false. Whole text replacement restores regular text with false, and Restart restores the original bold field. Font-face availability and original Flash metrics remain unverified.
- `AS3:property:flash/text/TextFormat.html#italic` — **partial**: Fixed current italic reads true and renders italic while default italic remains false. Whole text replacement restores regular text with false, and Restart restores the italic field. Font-face availability and original Flash metrics remain unverified.
- `AS3:constant:flash/text/TextFormatAlign.html#LEFT` — **partial**: LEFT selects the initial and restored left alignment in the actual changing field. This is limited to the observed uniform layout.
- `AS3:constant:flash/text/TextFormatAlign.html#CENTER` — **partial**: CENTER selects centered layout at logical width220. Exact Flash glyph rasterization/metrics are not established.
- `AS3:constant:flash/text/TextFormatAlign.html#RIGHT` — **partial**: RIGHT selects right-aligned layout at logical width300. Exact Flash glyph rasterization/metrics are not established.
- `as2:MovieClip.html#createTextField()` — **partial**: Append-depth creation returns a usable TextField with named receiver and788×64 box at64,474; its visible content is independent of AS3 fields. Arbitrary depth replacement and all historical return/error rules remain outside scope.
- `as2:TextFormat.html#TextFormat()` — **partial**: AS2 creates Arial18 RGB0x2ad4a5 uniform format, producing the visible legacy field. Other arguments/defaults/coercions remain unverified.
- `as2:TextFormat.html#leading` — **partial**: Setting leading4 participates in the visible AS2 multiline format; exact font metrics and other leading values are unverified.
- `as2:TextFormat.html#align` — **partial**: Setting left alignment participates in the visible AS2 uniform field. Other AS2 alignment values are not accepted from the AS3 cycle.
- `as2:TextField.html#setNewTextFormat()` — **partial**: Setting the initial insertion format before writing text yields the expected Arial18 green AS2 field. Later default changes and copied-default semantics require separate observed evidence.
- `as2:TextField.html#setTextFormat()` — **partial**: A whole-field AS2 TextFormat is applied to the existing legacy text and yields the visible uniform style. Range overloads and mixed-format runs remain unverified.
- `as2:TextField.html#text` — **partial**: The created legacy field receives and renders its own text and recovers it after Restart. Rich text, input and implicit coercion are not accepted.
- `as2:TextField.html#multiline` — **partial**: The created AS2 field reads multiline=true, uses its own wrapped content and remains unchanged across neighboring AS3 reflow. False/toggle and all newline forms are unverified.
- `as2:TextField.html#wordWrap` — **partial**: The created AS2 field reads wordWrap=true and renders2 observed lines inside its788-unit box. Other widths and nonwrapping behavior are unverified.
- `as2:TextField.html#getNewTextFormat()` — **partial**: The AS2 getter returns insertion color2806949(0x2ad4a5) on its created green field, matching current color. Default getter copy mutation/alias guarantees were not tested.
- `as2:TextField.html#getTextFormat()` — **partial**: The AS2 whole-field getter reports current color2806949 beside its actual green text and matching default. Range queries, copied-format mutation and mixed formatting remain unverified.
- `as2:TextFormat.html#color` — **partial**: The returned legacy current and default formats both report2806949(0x2ad4a5) beside actual green text. Mixed colors, separate default changes and invalid/coerced values are unverified.
- `AS3:property:flash/text/TextField.html#textWidth` — **partial**: The initial primary reports332.392578125 textWidth beside its rendered layout in the actual browser. This is recorded as the observed host metric only, without independent font-measurement, Flash or cross-engine numerical equivalence.
- `AS3:property:flash/text/TextField.html#textHeight` — **partial**: The initial primary reports54 textHeight beside two visible lines in the actual host profile. Exact historical font metrics and clipping behavior are not established.
- `AS3:method:flash/text/TextField.html#getLineText()` — **partial**: getLineText(0) returns the displayed39-character first line plus its public CR terminator. This explicit-newline first line is accepted; other lines, soft-wrap boundaries and Unicode ranges remain unverified.
- `AS3:method:flash/text/TextField.html#getLineLength()` — **partial**: The initial first-line length is40 UTF-16 units:39 printable characters plus CR, matching its returned public text. Other lines and surrogate/soft-wrap boundaries remain unverified.
- `AS3:method:flash/text/TextField.html#getLineOffset()` — **partial**: The initial displayed first line reports UTF-16 offset0. Later wrapped/newline offsets are unverified.
- `AS3:method:flash/text/TextField.html#getLineIndexOfChar()` — **partial**: Character0 maps to line0 in the displayed fixture. Wrapping/newline/surrogate boundary mapping and invalid indexes remain unverified.
- `as2:TextField.html#_width` — **partial**: The created AS2 field reads_width788 beside its own rendered logical box. The createTextField argument established this width; a later _width setter, transformed dimensions and reflow at other widths were not tested.
- `as2:TextField.html#_height` — **partial**: The created AS2 field reads_height64 beside its rendered box. Direct setter, overflow clipping and transformed dimension behavior are unverified.

### TRANSFORMS-HTML5-20261027T0502Z — Independent HTML5 acceptance: TRANSFORMS-HTML5-20261027T0502Z

accepted bounded affine color and lifecycle profile with targeted as2 rgb gap

Finite 2D affine/color profile only. Reflection, zero-scale transitions, singular/stale targets, extreme coercion/bounds, relative matrices, full native subclassing, color rounding and historical Flash behavior were not independently accepted. The card listener is dynamic and no semantic button entry appeared for it. Alpha paint/hit was checked; dynamic accessibility export, semantic clipping/bounds and screen/window mapping remain needs_review. No exact screenshot RGB equivalence is inferred from arithmetic: compositing, premultiplied transparent storage, browser scaling and color management affect pixels. Only one source sample was directly read; no full buffer byte proof. Direct AS3 BitmapData construction now has a positive16x16 transparent-buffer rendering case. Opaque buffers, coercion/limits and all uncalled methods remain outside this evidence. AS2 rgb is still missing despite its readback. The noncanonical .color extension does not close the canonical declaration. No class-wide promotion and no unsupported API is implemented merely by a diagnostic. Only IAB; engine/version not separately identified, original Flash and other engines not run (documented_only). Native image/Library/Text/Sources/D-008 and unsaved-document-dependent UI remain pending; the document was untouched. Resource-label fixture issue was not retested. Direct file URL refusal was not retried. Main is developing Vector/Dictionary/for-in/for-each next. Later sources inherit no pass from this frozen Transform snapshot.

- `AS3:property:flash/display/DisplayObject.html#transform` — **partial**: Actual bound transform facades control the skewed Sprite/linked MovieClip and two tinted Bitmap views through the Rust graph; returned geometry/color copies are detached. Source-driven Cycle, actual transformed card input and Restart all retain the observed intended behavior.
- `AS3:property:flash/geom/Transform.html#matrix` — **partial**: Finite local affine assignments render the nested skewed card. Mutating returned matrix.tx to 900 leaves live rig tx at 96. The actual concatenated matrix assigned after reparent preserves world pose and hit position; restored pose and Restart return original values. Only the observed 2D finite profile is established.
- `AS3:property:flash/geom/Transform.html#colorTransform` — **partial**: Actual finite assignments alter vector/text/Bitmap paint. Detached redOffset mutation to 90 leaves live offset 0; assigning offset 256 is caught with a clear range diagnostic and preserves the live display. Alpha multiplier/offset changes govern visible card paint and observed hits, and sibling bitmap tints remain distinct with the sampled source pixel unchanged. This is not full-buffer byte equality or historical color fidelity.
- `AS3:property:flash/geom/Transform.html#concatenatedMatrix` — **partial**: All eight observed world-matrix traces match independent affine arithmetic within 1e-10. Phase2 reparent preserves the phase1 world matrix and transformed-coordinate card listener; phase0 and Restart restore the initial matrix. Other coordinate/window/3D profiles remain unverified.
- `AS3:property:flash/geom/Transform.html#concatenatedColorTransform` — **partial**: Actual composed alpha readbacks follow 1/0, 0/0, 0/100 and 0.7/30 under the rig, correlated with visible paint and input. Reparent to root returns composed alpha to 1/0 and visibly drops inherited rig tint while retaining pose. Other composed members are not independently promoted by this test.
- `AS3:constructor:flash/geom/Matrix.html#Matrix()` — **partial**: Actual finite six-parameter matrices establish the rig skew and card translation; rendered poses and all traced coefficients match the independent affine oracle across the observed phases. Uncalled Matrix methods remain outside scope.
- `AS3:constructor:flash/geom/ColorTransform.html#ColorTransform()` — **partial**: Identity and finite eight-parameter constructors produce actual vector/text tints, two differently tinted shared-buffer views and alpha-offset reveal. Out-of-bounds offset 256 is diagnosed at bound Transform assignment, not constructor creation. Historical coercion/rounding and broader native slots remain unverified.
- `AS3:property:flash/display/DisplayObject.html#rotation` — **partial**: Actual rotation read/write adds 12 degrees to the already skewed rig; measured matrix preserves the column-angle cosine and visible linked-card pose/input. Reflection and zero-scale cases were not exercised.
- `AS3:property:flash/display/DisplayObject.html#scaleX` — **partial**: Actual rig scaleX read/write multiplies its value by 1.1 without discarding skew; measured coefficients match the independent oracle. Bitmap view scaleX=6 also produces the visible enlarged views; zero/negative scale transitions remain unverified.
- `AS3:property:flash/display/DisplayObject.html#scaleY` — **partial**: Actual rig scaleY read/write multiplies its value by 0.85 without discarding skew; measured coefficients match the independent oracle. Bitmap view scaleY=6 also produces the visible enlarged views; zero/negative scale transitions remain unverified.
- `as2:flash/geom/Transform.html#Transform()` — **partial**: AS2 new Transform(legacyClip) binds the dynamically drawn stripe and its actual matrix/color assignments affect visible paint. The canonical ColorTransform.rgb defect is recorded separately; no full AS2 native-object claim.
- `as2:flash/geom/Transform.html#matrix` — **partial**: Actual AS2 finite matrix assignment skews/places the stripe, then _rotation 4/8/0 changes its visible pose. Copy, concatenation and other AS2 Transform members remain unverified.
- `as2:flash/geom/Transform.html#colorTransform` — **partial**: Actual AS2 bound color assignment paints the stripe green and the noncanonical .color positive control paints it red. Canonical .rgb only creates a field and does not update offsets/paint; this independent defect limits compatibility without erasing the working finite transform assignment.
- `as2:flash/geom/Matrix.html#Matrix()` — **partial**: AS2 Matrix(1,0.06,0.2,1,68,488) supplies the finite affine value actually used to render the stripe; no uncalled Matrix properties or methods are inferred.
- `as2:flash/geom/ColorTransform.html#ColorTransform()` — **partial**: AS2 no-argument ColorTransform produces a value accepted by the bound Transform and renders the stripe through the existing noncanonical .color control. This constructor evidence does not implement the documented AS2 rgb property.
- `as2:MovieClip.html#_rotation` — **partial**: Actual AS2 _rotation values 4, 8 and 0 appear in visible stripe readouts and change its pose across Cycle phases. Other scalar/coercion boundaries remain unverified.
- `AS3:property:flash/geom/Matrix.html#a` — **partial**: Actual a reads from the concatenated Matrix match independent affine arithmetic across all eight observed poses. Evidence is limited to the observed finite skew/rotation/scale/translation matrices.
- `AS3:property:flash/geom/Matrix.html#b` — **partial**: Actual b reads from the concatenated Matrix match independent affine arithmetic across all eight observed poses. Evidence is limited to the observed finite skew/rotation/scale/translation matrices.
- `AS3:property:flash/geom/Matrix.html#c` — **partial**: Actual c reads from the concatenated Matrix match independent affine arithmetic across all eight observed poses. Evidence is limited to the observed finite skew/rotation/scale/translation matrices.
- `AS3:property:flash/geom/Matrix.html#d` — **partial**: Actual d reads from the concatenated Matrix match independent affine arithmetic across all eight observed poses. Evidence is limited to the observed finite skew/rotation/scale/translation matrices.
- `AS3:property:flash/geom/Matrix.html#tx` — **partial**: Actual tx reads from the concatenated Matrix match independent affine arithmetic across all eight observed poses. Detached tx mutation to 900 leaves live rig tx at 96. Evidence is limited to the observed finite skew/rotation/scale/translation matrices.
- `AS3:property:flash/geom/Matrix.html#ty` — **partial**: Actual ty reads from the concatenated Matrix match independent affine arithmetic across all eight observed poses. Evidence is limited to the observed finite skew/rotation/scale/translation matrices.
- `AS3:property:flash/geom/ColorTransform.html#redOffset` — **partial**: Returned AS3 color copy redOffset can change to 90 while live rig offset remains 0. Changing copy offset to 256 and assigning it raises a clear range diagnostic without changing live offset or visible/input behavior. Getter/setter evidence is limited to finite numeric values and bound-assignment validation.
- `AS3:property:flash/geom/ColorTransform.html#alphaMultiplier` — **partial**: Direct authored writes to 0 and composed readbacks accompany hidden card paint/no observed extra callback; with offset 100 the card becomes visible and accepts the next click. Actual composed value 0.7 under the phase1 rig returns to 1 after reparent. Other alpha and coercion cases remain unverified.
- `AS3:property:flash/geom/ColorTransform.html#alphaOffset` — **partial**: Direct authored writes 0 and 100 change the parent-transformed card from hidden to visible while multiplier stays 0; composed trace and actual card clicks agree. Phase1 offset 30 returns to 0 on reparent. Bitmap alpha-offset reveal is observed visually, without asserting per-pixel Flash parity.
- `as2:flash/geom/ColorTransform.html#rgb` — **missing**: Confirmed missing effect on frozen 852c8b7: assigning rgb=0xff0000 reads back 16711680 but leaves offsets 35/199/176 and the stripe visibly green, without a diagnostic. The same object through noncanonical color=0xff0000 yields offsets 255/0/0 and a red stripe. This is a reproduced silent custom-field failure, not implemented rgb support.
- `AS3:constructor:flash/display/BitmapData.html#BitmapData()` — **partial**: Direct positive AS3 new BitmapData(16,16,true,0xffeaf3ff) supplies the rendered two-view composition, subsequent fillRect edits and sampled getPixel32(2,5)=4284458705. Both independently tinted views remain visible through the transform family; Restart constructs fresh source data. This narrows the prior constructor needs-review state, without promoting all BitmapData members or all dimension/format profiles.
- `as2:flash/geom/ColorTransform.html#redOffset` — **partial**: Direct AS2 redOffset readback is 35 after the ineffective canonical rgb assignment and 255 after the noncanonical color positive control, matching visible green/red stripe states. Independent direct redOffset writes were not exercised.
- `as2:flash/geom/ColorTransform.html#greenOffset` — **partial**: Direct AS2 greenOffset readback is 199 after ineffective canonical rgb assignment and 0 after the noncanonical color positive control, matching visible stripe states. Independent direct greenOffset writes were not exercised.
- `as2:flash/geom/ColorTransform.html#blueOffset` — **partial**: Direct AS2 blueOffset readback is 176 after ineffective canonical rgb assignment and 0 after the noncanonical color positive control, matching visible stripe states. Independent direct blueOffset writes were not exercised.

- `as2:XML.html#loaded` — **partial**: Actual successful main document reports loaded=true initially, across edits and after Restart; failure/cancellation/explicit loaded writes remain unverified.
- `as2:XML.html#parseXML()` — **needs_review**: Direct public parseXML is exercised only with malformed input on a valid separate probe: status becomes-6 and serialization stays unchanged. - `as2:XMLNode.html#nodeType` — **partial**: Actual nodeType==1 filter selects item elements for visible legacy totals and rows; other node types and mutations are not inferred.
- `as2:XMLNode.html#nodeName` — **partial**: Actual nodeName=="item" distinguishes the catalog item elements from the namespaced metadata note. Name mutation and broader namespace lookup remain unverified.
- `as2:XMLNode.html#attributes` — **partial**: Actual id/stock reads render source rows; cloned attribute writes produce COPY-1/COPY-2 with stock 5 in exact logical serialized trees and visible text. Whole-map replacement and namespaced attribute mutation are outside scope.
