- `as2:XMLNode.html#appendChild()` — **partial**: Actual append retains the cloned wrapper: root.lastChild==cloned and cloned.parentNode==root are true. Serialized output matches expected appended detached-clone ordering, separately from AS3 copied insertion.
- `as2:XMLNode.html#lastChild` — **partial**: Actual lastChild readback identifies the just-appended same cloned wrapper in each legacy update; empty-container and unrelated ordering cases are unverified.
- `as2:XMLNode.html#parentNode` — **partial**: Actual appended clone parentNode is the same root wrapper during each update; removed-node lifetime and parent-null checks were not exercised.
- `as2:XMLNode.html#removeNode()` — **partial**: Actual removal of the old first item after appending its edited clone preserves three records with totals 13/14/13 and expected order/content. Restart restores the original DOM and the next update repeats once; return-value and detached-wrapper access remain unverified.

### host:authored-handler-lifecycle — Authored handler lifecycle

New placements queue authored Load handlers; frame entry queues frame scripts; ticks queue authored EnterFrame handlers; clicks bubble authored handlers from leaf through ancestor placements. Dispatch skips stale lifetime/source/frame entries and enforces a 4096 event budget.

Only four serialized event kinds (load, click, enter_frame, frame). Order is explicitly NeoFlash initial semantics, not Flash verified. Assigning AS2 onEnterFrame is silently custom state and is never invoked (separate finding). No dynamically registered listeners, capture/priority/default prevention or stop propagation. AS3 event bindings are not automatically available in lowered scripts.

Evidence: [crates/neoflash-core/src/runtime.rs](../../crates/neoflash-core/src/runtime.rs), [crates/neoflash-core/src/runtime.rs](../../crates/neoflash-core/src/runtime.rs), [crates/neoflash-core/src/runtime.rs](../../crates/neoflash-core/src/runtime.rs), [web/runtime.js](../../web/runtime.js)

### host:eventdispatcher-and-graphics — Eventdispatcher and graphics

Proxy exposes no EventDispatcher methods or Graphics object. addEventListener and graphics.beginFill are rejected with an unknown API diagnostic by the limited AS frontend.

Do not infer full Graphics absence from authored geometry support: rectangles/ellipses/paths can render, but runtime drawing API is absent.

Evidence: [web/runtime.js](../../web/runtime.js), [web/runtime.js](../../web/runtime.js), [crates/neoflash-script/src/actionscript.rs](../../crates/neoflash-script/src/actionscript.rs)

### host:instance-navigation-and-frame-queries — Instance navigation and frame queries

root/_root and parent/_parent return proxies; currentFrame and totalFrames return shared model data. Unmapped reads search custom state, then direct child by authored name. Proxy identity is scoped to JSON instance-ID path plus placement lifetime.

No getChildByName/getChildAt/addChild/removeChild/DisplayObjectContainer implementation. The root is the authored root scene; AS3 Stage/root loader semantics are absent. Legacy slash paths, _levelN, target-path string resolution and dynamic display tree are not provided.

Evidence: [web/runtime.js](../../web/runtime.js), [web/runtime.js](../../web/runtime.js), [crates/neoflash-core/src/runtime.rs](../../crates/neoflash-core/src/runtime.rs)

### host:numeric-display-properties — Numeric display properties

Six numeric properties map to Rust transform overrides. AS2 alpha/scale use percentages; AS3 uses scalar alpha/scale. AS2 setters Number-coerce before finite validation.

Root transform setter is rejected; only active non-root placements receive overrides. AS3 setters require finite JS numbers; alpha must be in [0,1]. This is bounded host validation, not proof of Flash coercion semantics. rotation 370 reads back 370; Adobe AS3 documents normalization to 10.

Evidence: [web/runtime.js](../../web/runtime.js), [web/runtime.js](../../web/runtime.js), [crates/neoflash-core/src/runtime.rs](../../crates/neoflash-core/src/runtime.rs)

### host:timeline-controls — Timeline controls

Six timeline methods are exposed on every instance proxy. Frames are one-based and string labels resolve before numeric strings; an independent MovieClip or root timeline can be controlled.

No AS3 scene parameter or scene model. Graphic/synchronized symbols do not have independent clocks. nextFrame/prevFrame clamp and stop. play/stop return a Boolean from WASM instead of documented void; observed play() => true. General Flash frame entry/reentrancy ordering is not established by differential tests.

Evidence: [web/runtime.js](../../web/runtime.js), [web/runtime.js](../../web/runtime.js), [crates/neoflash-web/src/lib.rs](../../crates/neoflash-web/src/lib.rs), [crates/neoflash-core/src/runtime.rs](../../crates/neoflash-core/src/runtime.rs)

### host:trace — Trace

Injected trace writes prefixed values to console and a 160-character String-coerced feedback line. AS compiler redirects trace to captured host binding.

Output transport and coercion are NeoFlash behavior. No original Flash coercion/output differential run.

Evidence: [web/runtime.js](../../web/runtime.js), [crates/neoflash-script/src/actionscript.rs](../../crates/neoflash-script/src/actionscript.rs)

### native-placement-facade — Native placement facade

MovieClip/DisplayObject/TextField-like behavior exists only on authored placement proxies; no general class constructor/type/complete inherited API claim.

Use only within the exact reviewed rule; no full Flash compatibility is claimed.

Evidence: [crates/neoflash-core/src/model.rs](../../crates/neoflash-core/src/model.rs), [web/runtime.js](../../web/runtime.js), [web/runtime.js](../../web/runtime.js), [web/runtime.js](../../web/runtime.js)

### shared-button-display-proxy — Shared button display proxy

Serialized Button symbol placements use the same transform/parent proxy. Seven AS2 properties are inferred from this shared route; no individual Button execution or Flash up/over/down/hit state support is claimed.

Use only within the exact reviewed rule; no full Flash compatibility is claimed.

Evidence: [crates/neoflash-core/src/model.rs](../../crates/neoflash-core/src/model.rs), [web/runtime.js](../../web/runtime.js), [web/runtime.js](../../web/runtime.js), [web/runtime.js](../../web/runtime.js)
